-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  TRO v10 — ADVANCED LUAU RUNTIME ANALYSIS & DEEP CAPTURE ENGINE
--  Target : Delta Executor (UNC / sUNC compliant executors)
--  Policy : LOCAL ONLY — no data ever leaves the device (request/Drawing unused)
--
--  v10 is a full re-engineering of TRO v9 into independent modules:
--    M01 Core    — config, ids (session/correlation/error), hashes, utils
--    M02 Errors  — Error Manager + Missing Data Summary
--    M03 Caps    — Capability Manager (runtime environment probes + fallbacks)
--    M04 Perf    — Performance Manager (frame-budget scheduler, FPS/mem, Light Mode)
--    M05 Ser     — Serializer + Chunker (full-text preservation, zero data truncation)
--    M06 Store   — Storage Manager (debounced verified flush, backup, Auto Save OFF)
--    M07 Rec     — Event Diagnostics + Timeline (states, markers, Correlation IDs)
--    M08 Hooks   — Capture Manager (namecall/newindex/Instance.new/loadstring/load/require)
--    M09 Queue   — stateful Queue Manager (PENDING/PROCESSING/COMPLETED/FAILED/REJECTED/RETRY)
--    M10 Scripts — Script Analysis & Dumper (source/decompiled/bytecode/metadata separated)
--    M11 Funcs   — Function & Event Mapping (states + call graph + dependency graph)
--    M12 Gui     — GUI Analysis Engine (full props, snapshots, diff, runnable code)
--    M13 Snap    — Snapshot Manager (lighting/player/camera environment snapshots)
--    M14 Scan    — chunked tree enumeration + input/nil/memory/guard scans
--    M15 Report  — Report Builder + Quality Engine + TOC + Manifest
--    M16 UI      — Control Center (draggable ScreenGui, tabs, toggles, report output)
--    M17 Tests   — built-in self-test suite (runnable from UI / TRO.runTests())
--    M18 Boot    — bootstrap, public API
--    M19 HttpSpy — تبويب HTTP بزر تشغيل/إطفاء (طافي OFF عند أول فتح):
--                  اعتراض بنفس تقنية Http Spy الأصلي بالضبط — hookfunction
--                  على request + replaceclosure + __namecall + خطافات game
--                  المباشرة + coroutine للرد — يلقط كل الروابط بما فيها
--                  pastefy و pastebin مع Status/Body (عرض فقط افتراضيًا)
--    M20 BMon    — زر الالتقاط: بزر تشغيل/إطفاء مثل Http Spy تمامًا (طافي
--                  OFF عند أول فتح). عند تفعيله يتصل بكل زر تلقائيًا (يشمل
--                  الواجهات nil) وكل ضغطة تُلتقط "بكل القوة" فورًا (يبدأ
--                  التسجيل تلقائيًا) + نسب أول رابط HTTP للزر (الأول فقط)
--    M21 Blk     — مضاد HTTPS: تبويب جديد بزر تشغيل/إطفاء (طافي OFF عند أول
--                  فتح). عند تفعيله يُحجب أي رابط http/https/ws فورًا (رد
--                  فاشل فارغ) فلا يُنفَّذ الكود الذي بداخله، بينما يظل Http
--                  Spy يلتقط كل محجوب بعلامة [BLOCKED] + v10.3 توسّع للـSpy:
--                  يلتقط github وكل ما كان يفلت (http_request/http.get/
--                  WebSocket/HttpPost/HttpService + إصلاح وسيط self)
--
--  Key guarantees:
--    * Auto Save starts OFF (locked) — nothing is written to disk until the
--      user presses Save (or explicitly enables Auto Save).
--    * No arbitrary source truncation: full texts are chunk-stored, linked by
--      one id, and verified by length + hash (reconstruction ratio is real).
--    * A full hex preview is NEVER presented as "full bytecode": bytecode is
--      stored as a complete binary file when possible, otherwise metadata +
--      integrity status only.
--    * A module failure never stops the others: every module init is isolated.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  MODULE TABLES — declared once, filled later (single-file module pattern)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Core, Errors, Caps, Perf, Ser, Store, Rec, Hooks, Queue
local Scripts, Funcs, Gui, Snap, Scan, Report, UI, Tests, Boot
local HttpSpy, BMon, HttpBlock   -- ⑥ M19 Http Spy · M20 Button Monitor · ⑧ M21 مضاد HTTPS

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M01 — CORE ENGINE
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Core = {}

Core.VERSION   = "10.3.0"      -- ⑧ v10.3: مضاد HTTPS + Http Spy يلتقط github وكل ما كان يفلت
Core.FILE_REPORT = "TRO_WOWWW.txt"       -- ⑤ ملف واحد فقط لكل المخرجات — لا مجلدات
Core.GUI_ROOT_NAME = "TRO10_CC"          -- our own UI root (excluded from captures)

-- ---- configuration (limits are protective, never silent: every overflow
-- ---- is counted and reported in Missing Data) --------------------------
Core.CFG = {
    MAX_HASHES      = 60000,   -- dedupe pool for record()
    MAX_TIMELINE    = 80000,   -- rotating (oldest dropped, dropped counted)
    MAX_GUI_INSTS   = 4000,    -- per GUI tree -> PARTIAL marker beyond
    MAX_GUI_ROOTS   = 300,     -- live GUI roots kept
    MAX_SCRIPT_QUEUE= 1000,    -- queue capacity per session (REJECTED beyond)
    MAX_GUI_QUEUE   = 400,
    MAX_FUNCS       = 3000,    -- function map entries
    MAX_FUNC_SRC    = 300,     -- functions with FULL decompiled body stored
    REMOTE_SAMPLES  = 12,      -- distinct arg samples per remote (samples, marked)
    MAX_ARGS        = 20,      -- args per sample (marked "+N more")
    MAX_STR_ARGS    = 4000,    -- per arg inside a SAMPLE (length always shown)
    INLINE_FULL     = 268435456, -- ⑤ سياسة الملف الواحد: النصوص الكاملة تُضمَّن (سقف 256 MB)
    INLINE_FULL_NOFS= 33554432,  -- when writefile is unavailable (32 MB inline)
    FRAME_BUDGET_MS = 6,       -- scheduler budget per frame
    OVERLOAD_N      = 3000,    -- events/sec before event-storm light guard
    MAX_NIL_INSTANCES = 8000,
    LIGHT_FPS_ON    = 25,      -- below this -> Light Mode
    LIGHT_FPS_OFF   = 40,      -- above this (5 windows) -> recover
    QUEUE_RETRY_MAX = 2,       -- retries before FAILED
    UI_REFRESH_S    = 1.0,     -- Control Center refresh interval
    UI_REFRESH_LIGHT= 2.5,     -- refresh interval while in Light Mode
    REPORT_DISPLAY_CAP = 60000,-- Report Output TextBox display tail cap
    -- ⑥ v10.1 — إعدادات الطابور + Http Spy + مراقبة الأزرار
    QUEUE_MIN_INTERVAL = 0.01,  -- أدنى فاصل بين خطوات الطابور (أقصى سرعة)
    QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL = 2.0,   -- أقصى فاصل (أبطأ سرعة)
    QUEUE_MAX_BATCH    = 8,     -- أقصى عدد عناصر تُعالج في الخطوة الواحدة
    HTTP_MAXLOG     = 400,      -- سجل Http Spy الدوار (الأقدم يُسقط ويُعَدّ)
    BTN_WINDOW      = 5,        -- نافذة الالتقاط "بكل القوة" بعد الضغط (ثوان)
    BTN_ASSOC_S     = 5,        -- مهلة نسب أول رابط HTTP للزر المضغوط (ثوان)
    BTN_MAX_ENTRIES = 200,      -- سجل ضغطات الأزرار الدوار
    BTN_MAX_BUTTONS = 1500,     -- أقصى عدد أزرار نتصل بها مباشرة
    BLK_MAXLAST    = 200,      -- ⑧ v10.3: آخر الروابط المحجوبة المعروضة (دوار) — مضاد HTTPS
}

-- feature toggles (Control Center "Capture" tab)
Core.FEATURES = {
    scriptDumper = true,
    guiCapture   = true,
    propWrites   = true,
    namecallSlow = true,   -- slow-path namecall capture (GetService/Http/...)
    incoming     = true,   -- OnClientEvent / Bindable / button connections scan
    memoryScan   = true,
    -- ⑥ v10.1 — ميزتان جديدتان، كلتاهما OFF عند الإقلاع (تفعيل من تبويبهما)
    httpSpy        = false,  -- Http Spy (M19) — مقفل افتراضيًا
    buttonMonitor  = false,  -- مراقبة الأزرار (M20) — مقفلة افتراضيًا
    -- ⑧ v10.3 — مضاد HTTPS (M21) — مقفل افتراضيًا مثل البقية
    httpBlock      = false,  -- حجب كل روابط الويب (رد فاشل فارغ) — تفعيل من تبويبه
}

Core.START = os.clock()
Core.INTERNAL = false       -- true while TRO's own code runs (hooks ignore us)
Core.CAPTURING = false      -- ③ التسجيل مغلق عند الإقلاع — يبدأ فقط بزر "تشغيل التسجيل"

-- ---- counters / statistics --------------------------------------------
Core.Stats = {
    truncations     = 0,    -- DATA truncations only (never display previews)
    timelineDropped = 0,
    hashRejected    = 0,
    recordsRejected = 0,
    lightModeOn     = 0,    -- how many times Light Mode engaged
    noiseSkipped    = 0,    -- ④ عناصر ضوضاء مفلترة (CoreGui/واجهات المنفّذ)
    savesAttempted  = 0,
    savesVerified   = 0,
}

-- ---- session + correlation + error identifiers ------------------------
do
    local seed = math.floor(Core.START * 1000) % 2147483647
    local function rnd(n)
        seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648
        return math.floor(seed / 65536) % (n or 16)
    end
    local hex = {}
    for _ = 1, 4 do hex[#hex+1] = string.format("%X", rnd(16)) end
    Core.SESSION_ID = "SID-" .. os.date("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S") .. "-" .. table.concat(hex)
end
Core._cid = 0
Core._eid = 0

function Core.nextCid()
    Core._cid += 1
    return string.format("C-%06d", Core._cid)
end
function Core.nextEid()
    Core._eid += 1
    return string.format("E-%03d", Core._eid)
end

-- ---- tiny utils --------------------------------------------------------
function Core.h32(s)
    if type(s) ~= "string" then s = tostring(s) end
    local h = 5381
    for i = 1, #s do
        h = ((h * 33) + s:byte(i)) % 4294967296
    end
    return h
end

function Core.round(x, d)
    local m = 10 ^ (d or 0)
    return math.floor(x * m + 0.5) / m
end

function Core.nowStr()
    return os.date("%H:%M:%S")
end

-- run f with the INTERNAL flag raised (our own traffic is ignored by hooks)
function Core.internal(f, ...)
    local prev = Core.INTERNAL
    Core.INTERNAL = true
    local ok, a, b, c = pcall(f, ...)
    Core.INTERNAL = prev
    return ok, a, b, c
end

-- true when inst belongs to TRO's own Control Center (excluded everywhere)
local OURS_CACHE = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function Core.isOurs(inst)
    if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then return false end
    local c = OURS_CACHE[inst]
    if c ~= nil then return c end
    local p, n, found = inst, 0, false
    while p and n < 14 do
        if p.Name == Core.GUI_ROOT_NAME then found = true; break end
        p = p.Parent
        n += 1
    end
    OURS_CACHE[inst] = found
    return found
end

-- ④ ضوضاء مرفوضة: واجهات Roblox المدمجة في CoreGui + واجهات المنفّذ نفسه.
-- لا تمسّ PlayerGui ولا منطق اللعبة إطلاقًا — قوة الالتقاط للعبة كاملة كما هي.
local NOISE_PATTERNS = {
    "robloxgui", "robloxscript", "playerlist", "devconsole", "contactlist",
    "teleport", "robloxload", "corescript", "mainmodule", "delta", "synapse",
    "script-ware", "scriptware", "arceus", "fluxus", "codex", "hydrogen",
    "solara", "cryptic", "executor",
}
-- ④+ علامات هوية لواجهات المنفّذ (دلتا وغيره) كما كانت عند الإقلاع.
-- ملاحظة مهمة: الحاوية نفسها (gethui) لا تُعلَّم — فقط أبناؤها الموجودون عند
-- الإقلاع. أي واجهة تنشأ بعد التشغيل (مثل واجهة السكربت المشفر الذي تراقبه)
-- تبقى مؤهلة للالتقاط بشكل طبيعي.
Core.EXEC_NOISE = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
Core.EXEC_NOISE_MARKED = 0

local NOISE_CACHE = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function Core.isNoise(inst)
    if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then return false end
    local c = NOISE_CACHE[inst]
    if c ~= nil then return c end
    local names, p, guard = {}, inst, 0
    local found = false
    while p and guard < 24 do
        if Core.EXEC_NOISE[p] then found = true; break end      -- ④ علامة هوية
        local okN, nm = pcall(function() return p.Name end)
        if okN and type(nm) == "string" then names[#names+1] = nm end
        p = p.Parent
        guard += 1
    end
    if not found then
        local inCore = false
        for _, nm in ipairs(names) do
            if nm:lower() == "coregui" then inCore = true; break end
        end
        if inCore then
            for _, nm in ipairs(names) do
                local low = nm:lower()
                for _, pat in ipairs(NOISE_PATTERNS) do
                    if low:find(pat, 1, true) then found = true; break end
                end
                if found then break end
            end
        end
    end
    NOISE_CACHE[inst] = found
    return found
end

-- ④ تُستدعى مرة واحدة عند الإقلاع (قبل بناء واجهتنا): تعلّم أبناء gethui()
-- الموجودين عند الإقلاع + واجهات CoreGui الأصلية المطابقة لأنماط الضوضاء.
function Core.markExecutorNoise()
    local marked = 0
    if Caps.has("gethui") then
        local okH, h = pcall(gethui)
        if okH and h ~= nil then
            local okC, kids = pcall(function() return h:GetChildren() end)
            if okC and type(kids) == "table" then
                for _, k in ipairs(kids) do
                    if not Core.isOurs(k) and Core.EXEC_NOISE[k] == nil
                        and typeof(k) == "Instance" then
                        Core.EXEC_NOISE[k] = true
                        marked += 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local okCG, cg = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if okCG and cg ~= nil then
        local okC, kids = pcall(function() return cg:GetChildren() end)
        if okC and type(kids) == "table" then
            for _, k in ipairs(kids) do
                if not Core.isOurs(k) and Core.EXEC_NOISE[k] == nil
                    and Core.isNoise(k) and typeof(k) == "Instance" then
                    Core.EXEC_NOISE[k] = true
                    marked += 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    Core.EXEC_NOISE_MARKED = marked
    return marked
end

-- ④ سجل أسماء واجهات الماب/اللعبة الموجودة قبل تشغيل التسجيل (أسماء فقط،
-- بدون بناء عميق). يظهر في قسم S07 — والواجهات نفسها تبقى قابلة للالتقاط
-- كاملة عند تشغيل التسجيل.
Core.BASELINE_GUI = { names = {}, total = 0 }
function Core.takeBaselineGuis()
    local LAYER = { ScreenGui = true, BillboardGui = true, SurfaceGui = true }
    local function walkContainer(container)
        if container == nil then return end
        local ok, kids = pcall(function() return container:GetChildren() end)
        if not ok or type(kids) ~= "table" then return end
        for _, k in ipairs(kids) do
            if Core.BASELINE_GUI.total >= 400 then return end
            local okC, cls = pcall(function() return k.ClassName end)
            local okN, nm = pcall(function() return k.Name end)
            if okC and LAYER[cls] and okN and type(nm) == "string"
                and not Core.isOurs(k) and not Core.isNoise(k) then
                Core.BASELINE_GUI.total += 1
                if #Core.BASELINE_GUI.names < 200 then
                    Core.BASELINE_GUI.names[#Core.BASELINE_GUI.names + 1] = nm .. " [" .. cls .. "]"
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local lp = Core.localPlayer()
    if lp then
        local okP, pg = pcall(function()
            return lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 2)
        end)
        if okP then walkContainer(pg) end
    end
    pcall(function() walkContainer(game:GetService("StarterGui")) end)
    return Core.BASELINE_GUI.total
end

-- ⑤ base64 (لتضمين البايتكود الكامل داخل ملف الإخراج الواحد — لا ملفات ثنائية خارجية)
local B64_CHARS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local function b64ch(v) return B64_CHARS:sub(v + 1, v + 1) end
function Core.b64encode(data)
    if type(data) ~= "string" then return "" end
    local out, n, i = {}, #data, 1
    while i + 2 <= n do
        local a, b, c = data:byte(i, i + 2)
        local v = a * 65536 + b * 256 + c
        out[#out+1] = b64ch(math.floor(v / 262144) % 64)
            .. b64ch(math.floor(v / 4096) % 64)
            .. b64ch(math.floor(v / 64) % 64)
            .. b64ch(v % 64)
        i += 3
    end
    local rem = n - i + 1
    if rem == 1 then
        local v = data:byte(i) * 65536
        out[#out+1] = b64ch(math.floor(v / 262144) % 64) .. b64ch(math.floor(v / 4096) % 64) .. "=="
    elseif rem == 2 then
        local a, b = data:byte(i, i + 1)
        local v = a * 65536 + b * 256
        out[#out+1] = b64ch(math.floor(v / 262144) % 64)
            .. b64ch(math.floor(v / 4096) % 64)
            .. b64ch(math.floor(v / 64) % 64) .. "="
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

-- safe instance property read
function Core.getProp(obj, k)
    local ok, v = pcall(function() return obj[k] end)
    if ok then return true, v end
    return false, nil
end

-- sanitize a name so it can be used inside a file name
function Core.safeName(s)
    s = tostring(s or "?")
    return (s:gsub("[^%w%-_%.%s]", "_"):gsub("%s+", "_"):sub(1, 60))
end

-- display-only preview (NEVER counted as data truncation)
function Core.preview(s, n)
    s = tostring(s or "")
    if #s <= n then return s end
    return s:sub(1, n) .. "…[preview " .. n .. "/" .. #s .. " chars]"
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
Core.Players = Players
Core.RunService = RunService

function Core.localPlayer()
    local ok, lp = pcall(function() return Players.LocalPlayer end)
    if ok then return lp end
    return nil
end

-- stable instance path expression (weak-cached) — nil-parented kept visible
local PATH_CACHE = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function Core.pathExpr(inst)
    if not inst then return "nil" end
    if inst == workspace then return "workspace" end
    if inst == game then return "game" end
    local cached = PATH_CACHE[inst]
    if cached then return cached end
    local parts, p, guard, ok = {}, inst, 0, true
    while p and p ~= game and guard < 48 do
        guard += 1
        local okN, nm = pcall(function() return p.Name end)
        local okC, cls = pcall(function() return p.ClassName end)
        if not (okN and okC and type(nm) == "string" and type(cls) == "string") then
            ok = false
            break
        end
        parts[#parts+1] = { n = nm, c = cls }
        p = p.Parent
    end
    local expr
    if not ok then
        expr = "nil--[unreadable]"
    elseif p ~= game then
        local top = parts[#parts] or { n = "?", c = "?" }
        expr = '(nilParent:"' .. top.n .. '"|' .. top.c .. ')'
    else
        expr = 'game:GetService("' .. parts[#parts].c .. '")'
        for i = #parts - 1, 1, -1 do
            local nm = parts[i].n:gsub("\\", "\\\\"):gsub('"', '\\"')
            expr = expr .. ':WaitForChild("' .. nm .. '")'
        end
    end
    PATH_CACHE[inst] = expr
    return expr
end

function Core.fullName(inst)
    local ok, p = pcall(function() return inst:GetFullName() end)
    if ok and type(p) == "string" and #p > 0 then return p end
    return Core.pathExpr(inst)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M02 — ERROR MANAGER
--  Every error carries: Error ID, section, type, message, time, operation,
--  and its impact on the report. Plus the Missing Data Summary.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Errors = {}

Errors.list = {}      -- {id, section, etype, msg, op, impact, t, wall}
Errors.dropped = 0    -- errors dropped once MAX reached (still counted)
Errors.MAX = 2000

Errors.missing = {}   -- {key, what, why, impact, count, first, last}
Errors.missingByKey = {}

-- impact: "none" (cosmetic), "report" (a report part degraded), "data" (captured data lost)
function Errors.log(section, etype, msg, op, impact)
    local e = {
        id = Core.nextEid(),
        section = tostring(section or "?"),
        etype = tostring(etype or "?"),
        msg = tostring(msg or "?"),
        op = tostring(op or "?"),
        impact = impact == "data" and "data" or (impact == "report" and "report" or "none"),
        t = os.clock() - Core.START,
        wall = Core.nowStr(),
    }
    if #Errors.list >= Errors.MAX then
        Errors.dropped += 1
        table.remove(Errors.list, 1)
    end
    Errors.list[#Errors.list+1] = e
    -- mirror important errors into the timeline so they are never invisible
    pcall(function()
        if Rec then
            Rec.tlSys("ERROR " .. e.id .. " [" .. e.section .. "] " .. e.etype .. ": " .. Core.preview(e.msg, 160))
        end
    end)
    return e
end

-- aggregated "what TRO could NOT record" entry (deduped by key, counted)
function Errors.missingData(key, what, why, impact)
    local m = Errors.missingByKey[key]
    if m then
        m.count += 1
        m.last = Core.nowStr()
        return m
    end
    m = {
        key = tostring(key),
        what = tostring(what),
        why = tostring(why or "?"),
        impact = impact == "data" and "data" or (impact == "report" and "report" or "none"),
        count = 1,
        first = Core.nowStr(),
        last = Core.nowStr(),
    }
    Errors.missingByKey[key] = m
    Errors.missing[#Errors.missing+1] = m
    return m
end

function Errors.countImpact(kind)
    local n = 0
    for _, e in ipairs(Errors.list) do
        if e.impact == kind then n += 1 end
    end
    return n
end

function Errors.stats()
    local byImpact = { none = 0, report = 0, data = 0 }
    for _, e in ipairs(Errors.list) do byImpact[e.impact] += 1 end
    return {
        total = #Errors.list,
        dropped = Errors.dropped,
        byImpact = byImpact,
        missingCategories = #Errors.missing,
        missingItems = (function()
            local n = 0
            for _, m in ipairs(Errors.missing) do n += m.count end
            return n
        end)(),
    }
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M03 — CAPABILITY MANAGER (Delta compatibility)
--  Nothing is assumed: every interface is probed at runtime, with safe
--  fallbacks. The full matrix lands in report section S02.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Caps = {}

Caps.map = {}         -- name -> { ok=bool, note=string }
Caps.EXEC = "unknown"
Caps.FS_ROUNDTRIP = "not_tested"

local function probe(name)
    local ok = false
    pcall(function()
        if type(_G[name]) == "function" then ok = true end
    end)
    -- some executors expose globals only via getgenv
    if not ok then
        pcall(function()
            if getgenv and type(getgenv()[name]) == "function" then ok = true end
        end)
    end
    Caps.map[name] = { ok = ok, note = ok and "OK" or "MISSING" }
    return ok
end

function Caps.has(name)
    local m = Caps.map[name]
    return m ~= nil and m.ok
end

function Caps.probe()
    local names = {
        -- gc / env / registry
        "getgc", "getreg", "getrenv", "getgenv", "getinstances", "getnilinstances",
        "getloadedmodules", "getconnections", "getcallbackvalue",
        -- hooks
        "hookfunction", "hookmetamethod", "getnamecallmethod",
        -- metatables
        "getrawmetatable", "setreadonly", "isreadonly",
        -- instances
        "gethui", "cloneref", "compareinstances",
        -- scripts
        "getscriptbytecode", "getscripthash", "getscriptclosure",
        "getrunningscripts", "getscripts", "getsenv", "getcallingscript",
        "getscriptfromthread",
        -- closures
        "checkcaller", "newcclosure", "clonefunction", "isexecutorclosure",
        "iscclosure", "restorefunction", "getfunctionhash", "loadstring",
        -- debug library (checked separately)
        -- filesystem
        "writefile", "readfile", "appendfile", "isfile", "isfolder",
        "listfiles", "makefolder", "delfile", "delfolder", "loadfile",
        -- misc
        "decompile", "setclipboard", "identifyexecutor", "request", "getcustomasset",
        -- NOT used on purpose (local-only policy): request, Drawing, getcustomasset
    }
    for _, n in ipairs(names) do probe(n) end

    -- debug library probes
    if type(debug) == "table" then
        for _, n in ipairs({ "getupvalues", "getconstants", "getinfo", "getproto", "getprotos" }) do
            Caps.map["debug." .. n] = { ok = type(debug[n]) == "function", note = "" }
            if type(debug[n]) == "function" then Caps.map["debug." .. n].note = "OK" end
        end
    end

    -- executor identification
    if Caps.has("identifyexecutor") then
        local ok, n, v = pcall(identifyexecutor)
        if ok and n then
            Caps.EXEC = tostring(n) .. (v and (" " .. tostring(v)) or "")
        end
    end

    -- filesystem roundtrip probe (write -> read -> verify -> delete)
    if Caps.has("writefile") and Caps.has("readfile") and Caps.has("isfile") then
        local probeFile = "TRO_v10_probe.tmp"
        local token = "TRO10-" .. Core.SESSION_ID
        local okW = pcall(writefile, probeFile, token)
        local verified = false
        if okW then
            local okR, back = pcall(readfile, probeFile)
            verified = okR and back == token
        end
        if Caps.has("delfile") then pcall(delfile, probeFile) end
        if verified then
            Caps.FS_ROUNDTRIP = "OK"
        else
            Caps.FS_ROUNDTRIP = "FAILED"
            Errors.log("Caps", "probe", "filesystem roundtrip failed", "Caps.probe", "report")
            Errors.missingData("fs_roundtrip", "verified file writes",
                "writefile/readfile roundtrip did not verify", "data")
        end
    else
        Caps.FS_ROUNDTRIP = "UNAVAILABLE"
        Errors.missingData("writefile", "saving reports to disk",
            "writefile not available in this environment", "report")
    end

    -- ⑤ لا مجلدات: كل المخرجات داخل ملف واحد TRO_WOWWW.txt
end

-- ---- safe wrappers (fallback + error accounting) -----------------------

function Caps.writeFile(path, data)
    if not Caps.has("writefile") then
        Errors.missingData("writefile", "file write " .. tostring(path),
            "writefile not available", "data")
        return false, "no_writefile"
    end
    local ok = pcall(writefile, path, data)
    if not ok then
        Errors.log("Caps", "write", "writefile failed: " .. tostring(path), "Caps.writeFile", "data")
    end
    return ok
end

function Caps.readFile(path)
    if not Caps.has("readfile") then return false, "no_readfile" end
    local ok, v = pcall(readfile, path)
    if not ok then return false, "read_failed" end
    return true, v
end

function Caps.folderExists(path)
    if not Caps.has("isfolder") then return false end
    local ok, r = pcall(isfolder, path)
    return ok and r == true
end

function Caps.decompileScript(s)
    if not Caps.has("decompile") then
        return false, "decompile_unavailable"
    end
    local ok, r = pcall(decompile, s)
    if ok and type(r) == "string" then return true, r end
    return false, (type(r) == "string" and r) or "decompile_failed"
end

function Caps.decompileFn(fn)
    if not Caps.has("decompile") then return false, "decompile_unavailable" end
    local ok, r = pcall(decompile, fn)
    if ok and type(r) == "string" then return true, r end
    return false, "decompile_failed"
end

function Caps.copy(text)
    if not Caps.has("setclipboard") then
        Errors.missingData("setclipboard", "clipboard export",
            "setclipboard not available", "report")
        return false, "no_clipboard"
    end
    return pcall(setclipboard, text)
end

function Caps.count()
    local ok, miss = 0, 0
    for _, m in pairs(Caps.map) do
        if m.ok then ok += 1 else miss += 1 end
    end
    return ok, miss
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M04 — PERFORMANCE MANAGER
--  Frame-budget scheduler + FPS/memory telemetry + automatic Light Mode
--  (below 25 FPS: non-essential UI updates stop, captured data is kept).
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Perf = {}

Perf.jobs = {}             -- scheduler jobs: fn() -> true when finished
Perf.fpsSamples = {}
Perf.memSamples = {}
Perf.light = false
Perf.lightSince = 0
Perf.slowOps = {}          -- {name, ms, t} for ops slower than SLOW_MS
Perf.SLOW_MS = 50
Perf.opCount = 0
Perf._fpsLowStreak = 0
Perf._fpsGoodStreak = 0
Perf._started = false

local BUDGET = Core.CFG.FRAME_BUDGET_MS / 1000

function Perf.addJob(fn)
    Perf.jobs[#Perf.jobs+1] = fn
end

-- chunked pass over a list: fn(obj) per item, done() at the end
function Perf.runPass(list, chunk, fn, done)
    if not list or #list == 0 then
        if done then pcall(done) end
        return
    end
    local i, n = 0, #list
    local maxStep = math.max(1, chunk or 100)
    Perf.addJob(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        i += 1
        while i <= n do
            local obj = list[i]
            local ok = Core.internal(fn, obj)  -- pcall + INTERNAL shield
            if not ok then
                -- per-item errors ignored on purpose (scan resilience)
            end
            if os.clock() - t0 > BUDGET or (i % maxStep) == 0 then break end
            i += 1
        end
        if i > n then
            if done then pcall(done) end
            return true
        end
    end)
end

-- measure one operation; slow ops are mirrored to the timeline
function Perf.op(name, fn)
    local t0 = os.clock()
    local ok, a, b, c = pcall(fn)
    local ms = (os.clock() - t0) * 1000
    Perf.opCount += 1
    if ms > Perf.SLOW_MS then
        Perf.slowOps[#Perf.slowOps+1] = { name = name, ms = Core.round(ms, 1), t = os.clock() - Core.START }
        if #Perf.slowOps > 200 then table.remove(Perf.slowOps, 1) end
        pcall(function() Rec.tlSys("SLOW OP " .. name .. " " .. Core.round(ms, 1) .. "ms") end)
    end
    return ok, a, b, c
end

-- fps window handler (also exposed for self-tests)
function Perf._onWindow(fps, now)
    Perf.fpsSamples[#Perf.fpsSamples+1] = math.floor(fps)
    if #Perf.fpsSamples > 240 then table.remove(Perf.fpsSamples, 1) end
    if fps < Core.CFG.LIGHT_FPS_ON then
        Perf._fpsLowStreak += 1
        Perf._fpsGoodStreak = 0
        if not Perf.light and Perf._fpsLowStreak >= 2 then
            Perf.light = true
            Perf.lightSince = now
            Core.Stats.lightModeOn += 1
            pcall(function()
                Rec.tlSys("LIGHT MODE ON (fps " .. math.floor(fps) .. ") — UI updates reduced, data kept")
                if UI and UI.setLightMode then UI.setLightMode(true) end
            end)
        end
    elseif fps > Core.CFG.LIGHT_FPS_OFF then
        Perf._fpsGoodStreak += 1
        Perf._fpsLowStreak = 0
        if Perf.light and Perf._fpsGoodStreak >= 5 then
            Perf.light = false
            pcall(function()
                Rec.tlSys("LIGHT MODE OFF (fps recovered " .. math.floor(fps) .. ")")
                if UI and UI.setLightMode then UI.setLightMode(false) end
            end)
        end
    else
        Perf._fpsLowStreak = 0
        Perf._fpsGoodStreak = 0
    end
end

function Perf.start()
    if Perf._started then return end
    Perf._started = true

    -- frame-budget scheduler
    Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        local i = 1
        while i <= #Perf.jobs do
            local ok, done = pcall(Perf.jobs[i])
            if not ok or done == true then
                table.remove(Perf.jobs, i)
            else
                i += 1
            end
            if os.clock() - t0 > BUDGET then break end
        end
    end)

    -- fps + memory telemetry
    task.spawn(function()
        local frames, last = 0, os.clock()
        Core.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            frames += 1
        end)
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            local now = os.clock()
            local fps = frames / math.max(now - last, 0.001)
            frames, last = 0, now
            pcall(Perf._onWindow, fps, now)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(5)
            local ok, kb = pcall(function() return collectgarbage("count") end)
            if ok then
                Perf.memSamples[#Perf.memSamples+1] = math.floor(kb)
                if #Perf.memSamples > 240 then table.remove(Perf.memSamples, 1) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function Perf.stats()
    local sum, mn, mx = 0, 99999, 0
    for _, f in ipairs(Perf.fpsSamples) do
        sum += f
        if f < mn then mn = f end
        if f > mx then mx = f end
    end
    if #Perf.fpsSamples == 0 then mn = 0 end
    return {
        fpsAvg = #Perf.fpsSamples > 0 and Core.round(sum / #Perf.fpsSamples, 1) or 0,
        fpsMin = mn == 99999 and 0 or mn,
        fpsMax = mx,
        memNow = #Perf.memSamples > 0 and Perf.memSamples[#Perf.memSamples] or 0,
        memMax = (function()
            local m = 0
            for _, v in ipairs(Perf.memSamples) do if v > m then m = v end end
            return m
        end)(),
        light = Perf.light,
        lightCount = Core.Stats.lightModeOn,
        jobs = #Perf.jobs,
        ops = Perf.opCount,
        slowOps = #Perf.slowOps,
    }
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M05 — SERIALIZER + CHUNKER
--  ser(): full property serializer for the report.
--  Chunker: full-text storage split into chunks linked by ONE id, verified
--  by length + hash on read — the core of the high reconstruction ratio.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Ser = {}

Ser.CHUNK = 48000
Ser.store = {}        -- id -> { len, hash, chunks = {n}, complete, note }
Ser.order = {}        -- insertion order (for stats/report)
Ser.totalBytes = 0

local function q(s)
    s = tostring(s)
    return '"' .. s:gsub("\\", "\\\\"):gsub("\n", "\\n"):gsub("\r", "\\r")
        :gsub("\t", "\\t"):gsub('"', '\\"'):gsub("%z", "\\0") .. '"'
end
Ser.q = q

local function ns(n)
    if n ~= n then return "(0/0)" end
    if n == math.huge then return "(1/0)" end
    if n == -math.huge then return "(-1/0)" end
    if n == math.floor(n) and math.abs(n) < 1e15 then return tostring(n) end
    return string.format("%.10g", n)
end
Ser.ns = ns

Ser.ser = function(v, d)
    d = d or 0
    if d > 6 then return "nil" end
    local t = typeof(v)
    if t == "nil" then return "nil" end
    if t == "boolean" then return tostring(v) end
    if t == "number" then return ns(v) end
    if t == "string" then
        if #v > 600 then return q(v:sub(1, 600) .. "…[len=" .. #v .. "]") end
        return q(v)
    end
    if t == "function" then return "function:" .. tostring(v) end
    if t == "thread" then return "thread:" .. tostring(v) end
    if t == "Vector2" then return string.format("Vector2.new(%s,%s)", ns(v.X), ns(v.Y)) end
    if t == "Vector3" then return string.format("Vector3.new(%s,%s,%s)", ns(v.X), ns(v.Y), ns(v.Z)) end
    if t == "CFrame" then
        local x,y,z,a,b,c,e,f,g,h,i,j = v:GetComponents()
        return string.format("CFrame.new(%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)",
            ns(x),ns(y),ns(z),ns(a),ns(b),ns(c),ns(e),ns(f),ns(g),ns(h),ns(i),ns(j))
    end
    if t == "Color3" then return string.format("Color3.new(%s,%s,%s)", ns(v.R), ns(v.G), ns(v.B)) end
    if t == "BrickColor" then return "BrickColor.new(" .. v.Number .. ")" end
    if t == "UDim" then return string.format("UDim.new(%s,%s)", ns(v.Scale), ns(v.Offset)) end
    if t == "UDim2" then
        return string.format("UDim2.new(%s,%s,%s,%s)",
            ns(v.X.Scale), ns(v.X.Offset), ns(v.Y.Scale), ns(v.Y.Offset))
    end
    if t == "Rect" then
        return string.format("Rect.new(%s,%s,%s,%s)", ns(v.Min.X), ns(v.Min.Y), ns(v.Max.X), ns(v.Max.Y))
    end
    if t == "EnumItem" then return "Enum." .. tostring(v) end
    if t == "Font" then
        local okW, wName = pcall(function() return v.Weight.Name end)
        local okS, sName = pcall(function() return v.Style.Name end)
        return string.format("Font.new(%s, Enum.FontWeight.%s, Enum.FontStyle.%s)",
            q(v.Family), okW and wName or "Regular", okS and sName or "Normal")
    end
    if t == "NumberRange" then return string.format("NumberRange.new(%s,%s)", ns(v.Min), ns(v.Max)) end
    if t == "NumberSequence" then
        local ps = {}
        for i, kp in ipairs(v.Keypoints) do
            ps[i] = string.format("NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(%s,%s,%s)",
                ns(kp.Time), ns(kp.Value), ns(kp.Envelope))
        end
        return "NumberSequence.new({" .. table.concat(ps, ",") .. "})"
    end
    if t == "ColorSequence" then
        local ps = {}
        for i, kp in ipairs(v.Keypoints) do
            ps[i] = string.format("ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(%s,%s)", ns(kp.Time), Ser.ser(kp.Value, d+1))
        end
        return "ColorSequence.new({" .. table.concat(ps, ",") .. "})"
    end
    if t == "PhysicalProperties" then
        return string.format("PhysicalProperties.new(%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)",
            ns(v.Density), ns(v.Friction), ns(v.Elasticity), ns(v.FrictionWeight), ns(v.ElasticityWeight))
    end
    if t == "TweenInfo" then
        return string.format("TweenInfo.new(%s,Enum.EasingStyle.%s,Enum.EasingDirection.%s,%s,%s,%s)",
            ns(v.Time), v.EasingStyle.Name, v.EasingDirection.Name,
            ns(v.RepeatCount), tostring(v.Reverses), ns(v.DelayTime))
    end
    if t == "Instance" then return Core.pathExpr(v) end
    if t == "table" then
        local ps = {}
        local n = math.min(#v, 25)
        for i = 1, n do ps[i] = Ser.ser(v[i], d+1) end
        if #v > 25 then ps[#ps+1] = "--…+" .. (#v - 25) end
        local cnt = 0
        for k, val in pairs(v) do
            if type(k) ~= "number" and cnt < 12 then
                cnt += 1
                ps[#ps+1] = "[" .. Ser.ser(k, d+1) .. "]=" .. Ser.ser(val, d+1)
            end
        end
        return "{" .. table.concat(ps, ",") .. "}"
    end
    return "nil--[[" .. t .. "]]"
end

-- ---- Chunker: full-text preservation ------------------------------------

-- store a full text under one id; returns meta {len, hash, chunks, complete}
function Ser.put(id, text)
    if type(text) ~= "string" then
        return nil
    end
    local len = #text
    local hash = Core.h32(text)
    local chunks = {}
    local i = 1
    while i <= len do
        chunks[#chunks+1] = text:sub(i, i + Ser.CHUNK - 1)
        i += Ser.CHUNK
    end
    if #chunks == 0 then chunks[1] = "" end
    local meta = {
        id = id,
        len = len,
        hash = hash,
        chunks = chunks,
        complete = true,
        note = nil,
        chunkCount = #chunks,
    }
    if Ser.store[id] then
        Ser.totalBytes -= (Ser.store[id].len or 0)
    else
        Ser.order[#Ser.order+1] = id
    end
    Ser.store[id] = meta
    Ser.totalBytes += len
    return meta
end

-- reassemble and VERIFY (length + hash): returns text, complete, note
function Ser.get(id)
    local m = Ser.store[id]
    if not m then return nil, false, "missing_id" end
    local text = table.concat(m.chunks)
    if #text ~= m.len then
        m.complete = false
        m.note = "length mismatch " .. #text .. " ~= " .. m.len
        return text, false, m.note
    end
    if Core.h32(text) ~= m.hash then
        m.complete = false
        m.note = "hash mismatch"
        return text, false, m.note
    end
    m.complete = true
    return text, true, nil
end

-- verify without building the string (cheap integrity check)
function Ser.verify(id)
    local m = Ser.store[id]
    if not m then return false, "missing_id" end
    local len = 0
    for _, c in ipairs(m.chunks) do len += #c end
    if len ~= m.len then
        m.complete = false
        return false, "length mismatch"
    end
    return true, nil
end

function Ser.meta(id)
    return Ser.store[id]
end

function Ser.stats()
    return {
        items = #Ser.order,
        bytes = Ser.totalBytes,
        completeItems = (function()
            local n = 0
            for _, id in ipairs(Ser.order) do
                if Ser.store[id].complete then n += 1 end
            end
            return n
        end)(),
    }
end

-- inline text for the report: FULL text when under the inline limit,
-- otherwise (only when a file copy exists / must exist) preview + reference.
-- Returns the text block and a flag whether the full text was inlined.
function Ser.inline(id, label, kind)
    local m = Ser.store[id]
    if not m then
        return "-- " .. tostring(label) .. ": (missing)", false
    end
    local text, complete, note = Ser.get(id)
    local limit = Caps.has("writefile") and Core.CFG.INLINE_FULL or Core.CFG.INLINE_FULL_NOFS
    local head = "-- [" .. tostring(label) .. "] id=" .. id
        .. " kind=" .. tostring(kind)
        .. " len=" .. m.len
        .. " hash=" .. m.hash
        .. " chunks=" .. m.chunkCount
        .. " integrity=" .. (complete and "COMPLETE" or ("PARTIAL(" .. tostring(note) .. ")"))
    if m.len <= limit then
        return head .. "\n" .. tostring(text), true
    end
    -- over the inline limit: the FULL copy still exists (chunks + file), the
    -- report shows a preview and an explicit reference — never fake completeness
    return head
        .. "\n-- [OVER_INLINE_LIMIT=" .. limit .. " — النص الكامل موجود داخل "
        .. Core.FILE_REPORT .. " عبر مخزن المقاطع؛ ما يلي معاينة فقط]"
        .. "\n" .. Core.preview(text, 8000), false
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M06 — STORAGE MANAGER
--  * Auto Save starts OFF (locked) — disk writes happen ONLY on manual Save
--    or after the user explicitly enables Auto Save.
--  * Debounced, never concurrent, write-verified (read-back + hash), with a
--    .bak of the last verified good report. DIRTY is NEVER cleared on failure.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Store = {}

Store.autosave   = false   -- ← LOCKED OFF at boot (user must enable)
Store.autosaveBy = nil     -- who enabled it ("user")
Store.busy       = false
Store.dirty      = false
Store.lastSave   = { attempted = false, ok = false, verified = false, t = 0, size = 0, reason = "?" }
Store.lastGoodReport = nil -- in-memory copy of the last VERIFIED good report
Store.MIN_AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL = 5
Store._lastAutoTry = 0
Store._loopStarted = false

function Store.markDirty()
    Store.dirty = true
end

function Store.setAutoSave(on, who)
    Store.autosave = on == true
    Store.autosaveBy = Store.autosave and (who or "user") or nil
    pcall(function()
        Rec.tlSys("AUTO SAVE " .. (Store.autosave and "ENABLED by " .. tostring(who or "user") or "DISABLED"))
    end)
    if Store.autosave and Store.dirty then
        task.spawn(function() pcall(Store.save, "auto-on-enable") end)
    end
    if UI and UI.refresh then pcall(UI.refresh) end
end

-- full verified save. reason: "manual" | "auto" | "test" | ...
function Store.save(reason)
    if Store.busy then
        return false, "busy"   -- never concurrent writes
    end
    Store.busy = true
    Store.lastSave.attempted = true
    Core.Stats.savesAttempted += 1

    local okBuild, report, meta = pcall(Report.build)
    if not okBuild or type(report) ~= "string" then
        Errors.log("Store", "build", tostring(report), "Store.save(" .. tostring(reason) .. ")", "report")
        Store.lastSave.ok = false
        Store.lastSave.verified = false
        Store.lastSave.reason = "build_failed"
        Store.busy = false
        return false, "build_failed"   -- DIRTY stays true
    end

    if not Caps.has("writefile") then
        Errors.missingData("writefile", "saving report to disk",
            "writefile not available — use Copy Report instead", "report")
        Store.lastSave.ok = false
        Store.lastSave.verified = false
        Store.lastSave.reason = "no_writefile"
        Store.busy = false
        return false, "no_writefile"   -- DIRTY stays true (v9 wrongly cleared it)
    end

    -- النسخة الجيدة الأخيرة محفوظة بالذاكرة فقط (⑤ ملف واحد: لا نسخة .bak على القرص)

    local okW = Caps.writeFile(Core.FILE_REPORT, report)
    if not okW then
        Errors.log("Store", "write", "writefile failed", "Store.save(" .. tostring(reason) .. ")", "data")
        Store.lastSave.ok = false
        Store.lastSave.verified = false
        Store.lastSave.reason = "write_failed"
        Store.busy = false
        return false, "write_failed"   -- DIRTY stays true
    end

    -- verification: read back and compare length + hash
    local okR, back = Caps.readFile(Core.FILE_REPORT)
    local verified = okR and type(back) == "string"
        and #back == #report and Core.h32(back) == Core.h32(report)

    if verified then
        Store.dirty = false                  -- ONLY cleared on verified success
        Store.lastSave = {
            attempted = true, ok = true, verified = true,
            t = os.clock(), size = #report, reason = tostring(reason),
        }
        Store.lastGoodReport = report
        Core.Stats.savesVerified += 1
        pcall(function() Rec.tlSys("SAVE OK (" .. reason .. ") " .. #report .. " bytes verified") end)
    else
        Errors.log("Store", "verify", "write verification failed (read-back mismatch)",
            "Store.save(" .. tostring(reason) .. ")", "data")
        Store.lastSave.ok = false
        Store.lastSave.verified = false
        Store.lastSave.reason = "verify_failed"
        pcall(function() Rec.tlSys("SAVE FAILED verification — DIRTY kept, will retry") end)
    end

    Store.busy = false
    if verified then
        return true, nil
    end
    return false, "verify_failed"
end

-- one autosave tick (parameterized for self-tests)
function Store._autoTick(now)
    if not Store.autosave then return false end
    if not Store.dirty then return false end
    if Store.busy then return false end
    if (now - Store._lastAutoTry) < Store.MIN_AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL then return false end
    Store._lastAutoTry = now
    pcall(Store.save, "auto")
    return true
end

function Store.startLoop()
    if Store._loopStarted then return end
    Store._loopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            pcall(Store._autoTick, os.clock())
        end
    end)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M07 — EVENT DIAGNOSTICS & TIMELINE  (+ recording core)
--  Buckets keep recording data; the timeline is a diagnostic log with
--  states (RUNNING/PAUSED/STOPPED), markers, and Correlation IDs.
--  Every record gets a stable CID; related events chain the same CID.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Rec = {}

Rec.tlState = "RUNNING"    -- RUNNING | PAUSED | STOPPED
Rec.timeline = {}
Rec.stateLog = {}          -- timeline state transitions (always recorded)
Rec.markers = {}           -- {id, label, t, cid}
Rec.SEEN = {}              -- dedupe hash -> entry
Rec.seenN = 0
Rec.recordRejected = 0
Rec._mid = 0

Rec.buckets = {
    INVENTORY = {},   -- every remote found in the game
    REMOTES    = {},  -- outgoing FireServer/InvokeServer/Bindables (samples)
    REMOTES_IN = {},  -- incoming OnClientEvent / Bindable connections
    HIDDEN     = {},  -- hidden callbacks (OnClientInvoke/OnInvoke/RenderStep)
    WRITES     = {},  -- property writes
    NEWS       = {},  -- Instance.new
    CALLS      = {},  -- sound/tween/humanoid/camera/... calls
    SERVICES   = {},  -- GetService/require/http/SetCore
    SIGNALS    = {},  -- GetPropertyChangedSignal
    DESTROYS   = {},  -- Destroy/Clone
    CONNECTIONS= {},  -- input/click/prompt/button connections
    LOADS      = {},  -- loadstring/load captures (full sources!)
    DYN        = {},  -- dynamic script entries queued from loadstring
    HTTP       = {},  -- ⑥ طلبات Http Spy (تُسجل فقط عند تفعيله — عرض صرف)
    HTTPBLK    = {},  -- ⑧ v10.3: الروابط المحجوبة بواسطة مضاد HTTPS (تجميع لكل رابط)
    BUTTONS    = {},  -- ⑥ ضغطات الأزرار الملتقطة بواسطة مراقبة الأزرار (M20)
}

-- timeline entry (state-aware): system + marker entries always pass
local function tlPush(ty, msg, cid, force)
    if Rec.tlState ~= "RUNNING" and not force then return false end
    local e = {
        t = os.clock() - Core.START,
        wall = Core.nowStr(),
        ty = ty,
        msg = tostring(msg),
        cid = cid,
    }
    if #Rec.timeline >= Core.CFG.MAX_TIMELINE then
        table.remove(Rec.timeline, 1)      -- rotating: oldest dropped
        Core.Stats.timelineDropped += 1    -- ...but always counted
    end
    Rec.timeline[#Rec.timeline+1] = e
    return true
end

function Rec.tl(msg, cid)         -- normal event
    return tlPush("EVT", msg, cid, false)
end
function Rec.tlSys(msg)           -- system line (always recorded)
    return tlPush("SYS", msg, nil, true)
end
function Rec.tlErr(msg, cid)      -- error mirror (always recorded)
    return tlPush("ERR", msg, cid, true)
end

-- ---- timeline controls --------------------------------------------------
Rec.controls = {}
function Rec.controls.pause()
    if Rec.tlState ~= "RUNNING" then return false end
    Rec.tlState = "PAUSED"
    Rec.stateLog[#Rec.stateLog+1] = { t = os.clock() - Core.START, wall = Core.nowStr(), from = "RUNNING", to = "PAUSED" }
    Rec.tlSys("TIMELINE PAUSED (events no longer logged; data buckets still record)")
    return true
end
function Rec.controls.resume()
    if Rec.tlState ~= "PAUSED" then return false end
    Rec.tlState = "RUNNING"
    Rec.stateLog[#Rec.stateLog+1] = { t = os.clock() - Core.START, wall = Core.nowStr(), from = "PAUSED", to = "RUNNING" }
    Rec.tlSys("TIMELINE RESUMED")
    return true
end
function Rec.controls.stop()
    if Rec.tlState == "STOPPED" then return false end
    local from = Rec.tlState
    Rec.tlState = "STOPPED"
    Rec.stateLog[#Rec.stateLog+1] = { t = os.clock() - Core.START, wall = Core.nowStr(), from = from, to = "STOPPED" }
    Rec.tlSys("TIMELINE STOPPED")
    return true
end
function Rec.controls.start()
    -- restart after a stop
    local from = Rec.tlState
    Rec.tlState = "RUNNING"
    Rec.stateLog[#Rec.stateLog+1] = { t = os.clock() - Core.START, wall = Core.nowStr(), from = from, to = "RUNNING" }
    Rec.tlSys("TIMELINE STARTED")
    return true
end
function Rec.controls.marker(label)
    Rec._mid += 1
    local m = {
        id = "M-" .. string.format("%04d", Rec._mid),
        label = tostring(label or "marker"),
        t = os.clock() - Core.START,
        wall = Core.nowStr(),
        cid = Core.nextCid(),
    }
    Rec.markers[#Rec.markers+1] = m
    tlPush("MARKER", m.id .. " " .. m.label, m.cid, true)
    Store.markDirty()
    return m
end

-- ---- record(): deduped bucket entry + timeline mirror --------------------
-- entry fields: line, count, first, last, cid, ctx, stack, samples
function Rec.record(bucket, catName, key, line, timelineLine, extra)
    if bucket ~= nil then Store.markDirty() end
    if timelineLine and Rec.tlState == "RUNNING" and #Rec.timeline < Core.CFG.MAX_TIMELINE then
        tlPush("EVT", timelineLine or line, nil, false)
    end
    if not bucket then return nil end
    local h = Core.h32(catName .. "|" .. key)
    local e = Rec.SEEN[h]
    if e then
        e.count += 1
        e.last = os.clock()
        return e
    end
    if Rec.seenN >= Core.CFG.MAX_HASHES then
        Rec.recordRejected += 1
        Core.Stats.hashRejected += 1
        return nil
    end
    Rec.seenN += 1
    e = extra or {}
    e.line = line
    e.count = 1
    e.first = os.clock()
    if e.cid == nil then e.cid = Core.nextCid() end   -- keep explicit chained cids
    Rec.SEEN[h] = e
    bucket[#bucket+1] = e
    return e
end

-- context snapshot (only taken for NEW entries by callers)
function Rec.context()
    local lp = Core.localPlayer()
    if not lp then return nil end
    local ok, ch = pcall(function() return lp.Character end)
    if not ok or not ch then return nil end
    local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local bp = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    local tools = {}
    if bp then
        for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if t:IsA("Tool") then tools[#tools+1] = t.Name end
        end
    end
    return string.format("hp=%s/%s pos=%s tools={%s}",
        hum and tostring(math.floor(hum.Health)) or "?",
        hum and tostring(math.floor(hum.MaxHealth)) or "?",
        hrp and Ser.ser(hrp.Position) or "?",
        table.concat(tools, ","))
end

function Rec.callStack()
    if not Caps.has("debug.getinfo") then return nil end
    local frames = {}
    for level = 2, 9 do
        local ok, info = pcall(debug.getinfo, level, "sln")
        if not ok or not info then break end
        frames[#frames+1] = string.format("%s:%s(%s)",
            tostring(info.source or "?"), tostring(info.currentline or 0), tostring(info.name or "?"))
        if #frames >= 6 then break end
    end
    if #frames == 0 then return nil end
    local scriptName = nil
    if Caps.has("getcallingscript") then
        pcall(function()
            local scr = getcallingscript()
            if scr then scriptName = Core.fullName(scr) end
        end)
    end
    return table.concat(frames, " <- ") .. (scriptName and (" [script:" .. scriptName .. "]") or "")
end

function Rec.reset()
    -- memory reset for Clear (files on disk are NEVER touched)
    Rec.timeline = {}
    Rec.stateLog = {}
    Rec.markers = {}
    Rec.SEEN = {}
    Rec.seenN = 0
    Rec.recordRejected = 0
    for k in pairs(Rec.buckets) do Rec.buckets[k] = {} end
    Rec.tlState = "RUNNING"
end

function Rec.stats()
    local b = {}
    for k, v in pairs(Rec.buckets) do b[k] = #v end
    return {
        timeline = #Rec.timeline,
        timelineDropped = Core.Stats.timelineDropped,
        state = Rec.tlState,
        markers = #Rec.markers,
        unique = Rec.seenN,
        rejected = Rec.recordRejected,
        buckets = b,
    }
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M08 — CAPTURE MANAGER (Global Hooks)
--  __namecall / __newindex via hookmetamethod + newcclosure (stable, does
--  not collide with other scripts). Instance.new, loadstring, load, require.
--  Every hook: pcall-shielded capture, original ALWAYS runs, our own traffic
--  skipped (INTERNAL + checkcaller + isOurs). One hook failing never blocks
--  the others.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Hooks = {}

Hooks.installed = {}    -- name -> true | (false, reason)
Hooks.OLD = {}          -- kept originals for restore/TRO API

-- event-storm guard (port of v9 tickOverload)
local evN, evT = 0, os.clock()
local OVERLOAD = false
local function tickOverload()
    evN += 1
    if evN >= Core.CFG.OVERLOAD_N then
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - evT < 1 then
            if not OVERLOAD then
                OVERLOAD = true
                Rec.tlSys("EVENT STORM -> arg sampling paused 2s (buckets keep counting)")
                task.delay(2, function() OVERLOAD = false end)
            end
        end
        evN, evT = 0, now
    end
end
Hooks.isOverloaded = function() return OVERLOAD end

-- should this call be captured at all?
-- (HOOK_TEST_MODE lets the self-tests exercise the loadstring hook even
-- though test calls come from our own executor thread)
local function capturable(inst)
    if Core.HOOK_TEST_MODE == true then return true end
    if not Core.CAPTURING then return false end          -- ③ التسجيل لا يعمل قبل التشغيل اليدوي
    if Core.INTERNAL then return false end
    if Caps.has("checkcaller") then
        local ok, own = pcall(checkcaller)
        if ok and own then return false end
    end
    if inst ~= nil and Core.isNoise(inst) then           -- ④ ضوضاء CoreGui/المنفّذ
        Core.Stats.noiseSkipped += 1
        return false
    end
    if inst ~= nil and Core.isOurs(inst) then return false end
    return true
end

-- ═══════════ 8.1 — __namecall ═══════════
local NAMECALL_FAST = {
    FireServer=true, InvokeServer=true, Fire=true, Invoke=true,
    FireAllClients=true, FireClient=true, InvokeClient=true,
    Play=true, Emit=true, Create=true, MoveTo=true, ChangeState=true,
    Jump=true, Activate=true, Unequip=true, Equip=true, Interpolate=true,
    LoadCharacter=true, Move=true, CreatePath=true,
    BindToRenderStep=true, UnbindFromRenderStep=true,
}

local REMOTE_STATE = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function buildArgs(n, ...)
    local args = {}
    local lim = math.min(n, Core.CFG.MAX_ARGS)
    for i = 1, lim do
        local a = select(i, ...)
        local ok, s = pcall(Ser.ser, a)
        if not ok then s = "nil--[[?]]" end
        -- args are SAMPLES: capped for readability, real length always shown
        if type(a) == "string" and #a > Core.CFG.MAX_STR_ARGS then
            s = Ser.q(a:sub(1, Core.CFG.MAX_STR_ARGS) .. "…[len=" .. #a .. "]")
        end
        args[i] = s
    end
    if n > Core.CFG.MAX_ARGS then
        args[#args+1] = "--…+" .. (n - Core.CFG.MAX_ARGS) .. " more args"
    end
    return table.concat(args, ", ")
end

local function captureRemote(self, m, prefix, ...)
    local st = REMOTE_STATE[self]
    if not st then
        st = { p = Core.pathExpr(self), n = 0, entry = nil }
        REMOTE_STATE[self] = st
    end
    st.n += 1
    local e = st.entry
    local isNew = false
    if not e then
        local line = string.format("%s:%s(...)", st.p, m)
        e = Rec.record(Rec.buckets.REMOTES, prefix, st.p .. "|" .. m, line, line, { samples = {} })
        if not e then return end
        st.entry = e
        isNew = true
        -- call-graph edge: who calls this remote?
        if Caps.has("getcallingscript") then
            pcall(function()
                local scr = getcallingscript()
                if scr then
                    Funcs.addEdge(Core.fullName(scr), st.p .. ":" .. m, "remote")
                end
            end)
        end
        local ctx = Rec.context()
        if ctx then e.ctx = ctx end
        local stk = Rec.callStack()
        if stk then e.stack = stk end
    end

    local samples = e.samples
    if not samples then samples = {}; e.samples = samples end
    local argstr
    if OVERLOAD and #samples > 0 then
        argstr = "(…)"
    elseif #samples < Core.CFG.REMOTE_SAMPLES or (st.n % 50) == 0 then
        local n = select("#", ...)
        argstr = "(" .. buildArgs(n, ...) .. ")"
        local dup = false
        for i = 1, #samples do
            if samples[i] == argstr then dup = true; break end
        end
        if not dup and #samples < Core.CFG.REMOTE_SAMPLES then
            samples[#samples+1] = argstr
        elseif dup then
            argstr = "(same as sample)"
        end
    else
        argstr = "(…)"
    end
    if not isNew then
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.REMOTES, prefix, st.p .. "|" .. m, e.line,
            string.format("%s:%s%s", st.p, m, argstr))
    end
end

local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")

local function captureNamecall(self, m, ...)
    local c = self.ClassName

    if m == "FireServer" or m == "InvokeServer" then
        if (m == "FireServer" and (c == "RemoteEvent" or c == "UnreliableRemoteEvent"))
            or (m == "InvokeServer" and c == "RemoteFunction") then
            captureRemote(self, m, "remote", ...)
        end
    elseif m == "Fire" or m == "Invoke" then
        if (m == "Fire" and c == "BindableEvent") or (m == "Invoke" and c == "BindableFunction") then
            captureRemote(self, m, "bindable", ...)
        end
    elseif m == "FireAllClients" or m == "FireClient" or m == "InvokeClient" then
        if c == "RemoteEvent" or c == "UnreliableRemoteEvent" or c == "RemoteFunction" then
            captureRemote(self, m, "remote_" .. m, ...)
        end
    elseif m == "Play" then
        if c == "Sound" then
            local p = Core.pathExpr(self)
            local line = string.format("%s:Play()  -- SoundId=%s", p, Ser.q(tostring(self.SoundId)))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Sound.Play", p, line, line)
        elseif c == "AnimationTrack" then
            local anim = self.Animation
            local id = anim and tostring(anim.AnimationId) or "?"
            local line = string.format("-- AnimationTrack:Play() id=%s speed=%s weight=%s",
                Ser.q(id), tostring(self.Speed), tostring(self.WeightCurrent))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Anim.Play", id .. "|" .. tostring(self.Speed), line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "Emit" then
        if c == "ParticleEmitter" then
            local p = Core.pathExpr(self)
            local line = string.format("%s:Emit(%s)", p, tostring((...)))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Emit", p, line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "Create" then
        if self == TweenService then
            local target, info, goals = ...
            if target and info and goals then
                local parts = {}
                for k, v in pairs(goals) do
                    parts[#parts+1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. Ser.ser(v)
                end
                local line = string.format("TweenService:Create(%s,%s,{%s}):Play()",
                    Core.pathExpr(target), Ser.ser(info), table.concat(parts, ","))
                Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Tween", Core.pathExpr(target), line, line)
            end
        elseif self == PathfindingService then
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Path", "-- PathfindingService:CreatePath(...)", nil, nil)
        end
    elseif m == "MoveTo" then
        if c == "Humanoid" then
            local line = string.format("-- %s:MoveTo(%s)", Core.pathExpr(self.Parent), Ser.ser((...)))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Humanoid.MoveTo", Core.pathExpr(self.Parent), line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "ChangeState" then
        if c == "Humanoid" then
            local line = string.format("-- %s:ChangeState(%s)", Core.pathExpr(self.Parent), tostring((...)))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Humanoid.ChangeState", Core.pathExpr(self.Parent), line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "Jump" then
        if c == "Humanoid" then
            local line = string.format("-- %s:Jump()", Core.pathExpr(self.Parent))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Humanoid.Jump", Core.pathExpr(self.Parent), line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "Activate" then
        if c == "Tool" then
            local line = string.format("-- %s:Activate()", Core.pathExpr(self))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Tool.Activate", Core.pathExpr(self), line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "Equip" or m == "Unequip" then
        if c == "Tool" then
            local line = string.format("-- %s:%s()", Core.pathExpr(self), m)
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Tool." .. m, Core.pathExpr(self), line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "Interpolate" then
        if c == "Camera" then
            local target, duration, style, dir = ...
            local line = string.format("-- Camera:Interpolate(%s, %s, %s, %s)",
                Ser.ser(target), tostring(duration), tostring(style), tostring(dir))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Camera.Interpolate", "camera", line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "LoadCharacter" then
        if c == "Player" then
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Player.LoadCharacter", "-- Player:LoadCharacter()", nil, nil)
        end
    elseif m == "Move" then
        if c == "Player" then
            local line = string.format("-- Player:Move(%s)", Ser.ser((...)))
            Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Player.Move", "player", line, line)
        end
    elseif m == "BindToRenderStep" then
        local name, priority = ...
        local line = string.format("-- RunService:BindToRenderStep(%s, %s)",
            Ser.q(tostring(name)), tostring(priority))
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "RenderStep.Bind", tostring(name) .. "|" .. tostring(priority), line, line)
    elseif m == "UnbindFromRenderStep" then
        local line = string.format("-- RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(%s)", Ser.q(tostring((...))))
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "RenderStep.Unbind", tostring((...)), line, line)
    end
end

local function captureNamecallSlow(self, m, ...)
    if m == "GetService" then
        local line = "-- GetService(" .. Ser.q(tostring((...))) .. ")"
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.SERVICES, "svc", tostring((...)), line, line)
    elseif m == "GetPropertyChangedSignal" then
        local p = Core.pathExpr(self)
        local line = "-- " .. p .. ":GetPropertyChangedSignal(" .. Ser.q(tostring((...))) .. ")"
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.SIGNALS, "sig", p .. "|" .. tostring((...)), line, line)
    elseif m == "GetAsync" or m == "PostAsync" or m == "RequestAsync" or m == "GetProductInfo" then
        local line = string.format("-- HttpService/Marketplace:%s(%s)", m, tostring((...)))
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.SERVICES, "http", m .. "|" .. tostring((...)), line, line)
    elseif m == "SetCore" or m == "SetCoreGuiEnabled" then
        local line = string.format("-- StarterGui:%s(%s, %s)", m, tostring((...)), tostring(select(2, ...)))
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.SERVICES, "setcore", m .. "|" .. tostring((...)), line, line)
    elseif m == "Kick" then
        local line = string.format("-- Player:Kick(%s)", Ser.q(tostring((...))))
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.CALLS, "Player.Kick", line, line, line)
    elseif m == "Destroy" or m == "Clone" then
        local p = Core.pathExpr(self)
        local line = "-- " .. p .. ":" .. m .. "()"
        local bucket = m == "Destroy" and Rec.buckets.DESTROYS or Rec.buckets.CALLS
        Rec.record(bucket, m, p, line, line)
    end
end

local function installNamecall()
    if not (Caps.has("hookmetamethod") and Caps.has("getnamecallmethod")) then
        Hooks.installed.namecall = "missing hookmetamethod/getnamecallmethod"
        Errors.missingData("hookmetamethod", "__namecall capture",
            "hookmetamethod or getnamecallmethod unavailable", "data")
        return
    end
    local oldNamecall
    local function hookNamecall(self, ...)
        local m = getnamecallmethod()
        if typeof(self) ~= "Instance" then
            return oldNamecall(self, ...)
        end
        tickOverload()
        if capturable(self) then
            if NAMECALL_FAST[m] then
                pcall(captureNamecall, self, m, ...)
            elseif Core.FEATURES.namecallSlow and not OVERLOAD then
                pcall(captureNamecallSlow, self, m, ...)
            end
        end
        return oldNamecall(self, ...)
    end
    if Caps.has("newcclosure") then hookNamecall = newcclosure(hookNamecall) end
    oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", hookNamecall)
    Hooks.OLD.namecall = oldNamecall
    Hooks.installed.namecall = true
end

-- ═══════════ 8.2 — __newindex (property writes) ═══════════
local WATCHED = {
    RemoteEvent     = { Name=true, Parent=true },
    UnreliableRemoteEvent = { Name=true, Parent=true },
    RemoteFunction  = { Name=true, Parent=true, OnClientInvoke=true },
    BindableEvent   = { Name=true, Parent=true },
    BindableFunction= { Name=true, Parent=true, OnInvoke=true },
    Humanoid        = { Health=true, MaxHealth=true, WalkSpeed=true, JumpPower=true,
                        JumpHeight=true, DisplayName=true, AutoRotate=true, HipHeight=true },
    TextLabel       = { Text=true },
    TextButton      = { Text=true },
    TextBox         = { Text=true },
    ImageLabel      = { Image=true },
    ImageButton     = { Image=true },
    Frame           = { Visible=true, Size=true, Position=true, BackgroundColor3=true,
                        BackgroundTransparency=true },
    ViewportFrame   = { ImageColor3=true, ImageTransparency=true },
    CanvasGroup     = { GroupTransparency=true, GroupColor3=true },
    BillboardGui    = { Enabled=true, Size=true, AlwaysOnTop=true, StudsOffset=true },
    SurfaceGui      = { Enabled=true, Face=true, AlwaysOnTop=true },
    Sound           = { SoundId=true, Volume=true, PlaybackSpeed=true, Looped=true, TimePosition=true },
    ParticleEmitter = { Enabled=true, Rate=true, Texture=true, Color=true, Size=true, Transparency=true },
    Beam            = { Enabled=true, Texture=true, Color=true, Width0=true, Width1=true },
    Trail           = { Enabled=true, Texture=true, Color=true, WidthScale=true },
    Highlight       = { Enabled=true, FillColor=true, OutlineColor=true, FillTransparency=true, OutlineTransparency=true },
    Camera          = { CFrame=true, FieldOfView=true, CameraType=true, CameraSubject=true },
    Tool            = { Name=true, Parent=true, Enabled=true, CanBeDropped=true },
    ProximityPrompt = { Enabled=true, ActionText=true, ObjectText=true, HoldDuration=true, MaxActivationDistance=true },
    ClickDetector   = { MaxActivationDistance=true },
    IntValue        = { Value=true },
    NumberValue     = { Value=true },
    StringValue     = { Value=true },
    BoolValue       = { Value=true },
    ObjectValue     = { Value=true },
    CFrameValue     = { Value=true },
    Vector3Value    = { Value=true },
    PointLight      = { Color=true, Brightness=true, Range=true, Enabled=true },
    SpotLight       = { Color=true, Brightness=true, Range=true, Angle=true, Enabled=true },
    SurfaceLight    = { Color=true, Brightness=true, Range=true, Angle=true, Enabled=true },
    Atmosphere      = { Density=true, Haze=true, Color=true, Decay=true, Glare=true },
    Sky             = { SkyboxBk=true, SkyboxDn=true, SkyboxFt=true, SkyboxLf=true, SkyboxRt=true, SkyboxUp=true, StarCount=true },
    BloomEffect     = { Enabled=true, Intensity=true, Size=true, Threshold=true },
    BlurEffect      = { Enabled=true, Size=true },
    ColorCorrectionEffect = { Enabled=true, Brightness=true, Contrast=true, Saturation=true, TintColor=true },
    SunRaysEffect   = { Enabled=true, Intensity=true, Spread=true },
    DepthOfFieldEffect = { Enabled=true, FarIntensity=true, FocusDistance=true, NearIntensity=true },
}

local WRITE_SEEN = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local writeMuted = false
local writeN, writeW = 0, os.clock()

local function installNewindex()
    if not Caps.has("hookmetamethod") then
        Hooks.installed.newindex = "missing hookmetamethod"
        Errors.missingData("hookmetamethod", "__newindex capture",
            "hookmetamethod unavailable", "data")
        return
    end
    local oldIdx
    local function hookNewindex(self, key, value)
        if typeof(self) ~= "Instance" then
            return oldIdx(self, key, value)
        end
        local k = key
        if type(k) ~= "string" then k = tostring(k) end

        -- hidden callbacks are ALWAYS captured (regardless of features/mute)
        if (k == "OnClientInvoke" or k == "OnInvoke") and capturable(self) then
            pcall(function()
                local p = Core.pathExpr(self)
                local line = string.format("-- [HIDDEN] %s.%s = %s", p, k, tostring(value))
                Rec.record(Rec.buckets.HIDDEN, "hidden", p .. "|" .. k, line, line)
            end)
            return oldIdx(self, key, value)
        end

        if writeMuted or not capturable(self) or not Core.FEATURES.propWrites then
            return oldIdx(self, key, value)
        end

        local seenProps = WRITE_SEEN[self]
        if seenProps == nil then
            seenProps = {}
            WRITE_SEEN[self] = seenProps
        end
        local e = seenProps[k]
        if type(e) == "table" then
            e.count += 1
            return oldIdx(self, key, value)
        end

        local ok = false
        local w = WATCHED[self.ClassName]
        ok = w ~= nil and w[k] == true
        if ok then
            pcall(function()
                local p = Core.pathExpr(self)
                local line = string.format("%s.%s = %s", p, k, Ser.ser(value))
                local ent = Rec.record(Rec.buckets.WRITES, "write", p .. "|" .. k, line, line)
                if ent then seenProps[k] = ent end
            end)
            -- write-storm protection
            if not writeMuted then
                writeN += 1
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - writeW > 1 then
                    if writeN > 5000 then
                        writeMuted = true
                        Rec.tlSys("WRITE STORM -> property capture muted 2s")
                        task.delay(2, function() writeMuted = false end)
                    end
                    writeN, writeW = 0, now
                end
            end
        end
        return oldIdx(self, key, value)
    end
    if Caps.has("newcclosure") then hookNewindex = newcclosure(hookNewindex) end
    oldIdx = hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", hookNewindex)
    Hooks.OLD.newindex = oldIdx
    Hooks.installed.newindex = true
end

-- ═══════════ 8.3 — Instance.new ═══════════
local function installInstanceNew()
    local okReady = pcall(function()
        local oldNew = Instance.new
        local function hookNew(cls, parent)
            local inst = oldNew(cls, parent)
            if capturable(parent) then
                pcall(function()
                    local parentPath = parent and Core.pathExpr(parent) or "nil"
                    local line = "Instance.new(" .. Ser.q(tostring(cls)) .. ")"
                        .. (parent and ("  -- parent=" .. parentPath) or "")
                    Rec.record(Rec.buckets.NEWS, "new",
                        tostring(cls) .. "|" .. parentPath, line, line)
                end)
            end
            return inst
        end
        if Caps.has("newcclosure") then hookNew = newcclosure(hookNew) end
        local okSet = pcall(function()
            if Caps.has("setreadonly") and Caps.has("isreadonly") then
                if isreadonly(Instance) then setreadonly(Instance, false) end
            end
            Instance.new = hookNew
        end)
        if not okSet then
            Hooks.installed.instanceNew = "Instance table is sealed"
            Errors.missingData("instance_new_hook", "Instance.new capture",
                "Instance table could not be modified", "data")
            return
        end
        Hooks.OLD.instanceNew = oldNew
        Hooks.installed.instanceNew = true
    end)
    if not okReady then
        Hooks.installed.instanceNew = "install error"
    end
end

-- ═══════════ 8.4 — loadstring / load (FULL source interception) ═══════════
-- The #1 v10 feature: every dynamically-loaded chunk has its FULL source
-- captured (chunk-stored, hash-verified) and queued as a dynamic script.
Hooks._LS_COUNT = 0

function Hooks.captureLoad(kind, src, chunkname, parentCid)
    if type(src) ~= "string" or #src == 0 then return nil end
    if #src > 8 * 1024 * 1024 then
        -- hard sanity ceiling (8 MB): refuse to store, but REPORT the refusal
        Errors.missingData("loadstring_huge", kind .. " source (" .. #src .. " bytes)",
            "source exceeds the 8 MB sanity ceiling", "data")
        return nil
    end
    Hooks._LS_COUNT += 1
    local id = string.format("LS-%04d", Hooks._LS_COUNT)
    local meta = Ser.put(id, src)   -- FULL text, chunked, no truncation
    if not meta then return nil end
    local cid = parentCid or Core.nextCid()
    local name = tostring(chunkname or (kind .. "#" .. Hooks._LS_COUNT))
    local line = string.format("-- [%s] %s len=%d hash=%d chunks=%d",
        kind, name, meta.len, meta.hash, meta.chunkCount)
    Rec.record(Rec.buckets.LOADS, kind, id, line, line, { srcId = id, name = name, cid = cid })
    Rec.tl(kind .. " captured: " .. Core.preview(name, 80) .. " (" .. meta.len .. " bytes)", cid)

    -- queue it as a dynamic script entry (source already in the chunk store)
    if Core.FEATURES.scriptDumper then
        pcall(function() Scripts.queueDynamic(id, name, kind, cid) end)
    end
    return id
end

local function wrapLoader(name)
    local g = _G
    if Caps.has("getgenv") then
        pcall(function() g = getgenv() end)
    end
    local old = rawget(g, name)
    if type(old) ~= "function" then
        Hooks.installed[name] = "not a function"
        return
    end
    local function hook(src, chunkname)
        if capturable(nil) then
            pcall(Hooks.captureLoad, name, src, chunkname, nil)
        end
        return old(src, chunkname)
    end
    local wrapped = hook
    if Caps.has("newcclosure") then wrapped = newcclosure(hook) end
    local okSet = pcall(function() g[name] = wrapped end)
    if okSet then
        Hooks.OLD[name] = old
        Hooks.installed[name] = true
    else
        Hooks.installed[name] = "could not replace global"
        Errors.missingData(name .. "_hook", name .. " source interception",
            "global " .. name .. " could not be replaced", "data")
    end
end

-- ═══════════ 8.5 — require (dependency graph) ═══════════
local function installRequire()
    if not Caps.has("hookfunction") then
        Hooks.installed.require = "missing hookfunction"
        Errors.missingData("hookfunction", "require() dependency capture",
            "hookfunction unavailable", "data")
        return
    end
    local oldRequire = require
    local function hookRequire(mod)
        if capturable(nil) then
            pcall(function()
                local what = typeof(mod) == "Instance" and Core.pathExpr(mod) or tostring(mod)
                local line = "-- require(" .. what .. ")"
                Rec.record(Rec.buckets.SERVICES, "require", tostring(what), line, line)
                -- dependency-graph edge: caller script -> module
                if Caps.has("getcallingscript") then
                    local scr = getcallingscript()
                    if scr then
                        Funcs.addDep(Core.fullName(scr), tostring(what))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        return oldRequire(mod)
    end
    if Caps.has("newcclosure") then hookRequire = newcclosure(hookRequire) end
    local ok = pcall(function() hookfunction(require, hookRequire) end)
    if ok then
        Hooks.installed.require = true
    else
        Hooks.installed.require = "hookfunction failed"
        Errors.missingData("require_hook", "require() dependency capture",
            "hookfunction(require) failed", "data")
    end
end

-- ═══════════ 8.6 — install all (isolated) ═══════════
function Hooks.install()
    if Hooks._didInstall then return end                 -- ③ تُركَّب مرة واحدة عند أول تشغيل
    Hooks._didInstall = true
    local installers = {
        { "namecall", installNamecall },
        { "newindex", installNewindex },
        { "instanceNew", installInstanceNew },
        { "loadstring", function() wrapLoader("loadstring") end },
        { "load", function() wrapLoader("load") end },
        { "require", installRequire },
    }
    for _, item in ipairs(installers) do
        local name, fn = item[1], item[2]
        local ok, err = pcall(fn)
        if not ok then
            Hooks.installed[name] = "error: " .. tostring(err)
            Errors.log("Hooks", "install", tostring(err), "install:" .. name, "data")
        end
    end
end

function Hooks.stats()
    local t = {}
    for name, v in pairs(Hooks.installed) do
        t[#t+1] = name .. "=" .. (v == true and "OK" or tostring(v))
    end
    table.sort(t)
    return table.concat(t, " ")
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M09 — QUEUE MANAGER (stateful)
--  States: PENDING → PROCESSING → COMPLETED
--                  ↘ FAILED (after RETRY_PENDING × QUEUE_RETRY_MAX)
--          REJECTED (capacity full — caller is told, so NOTHING is lost:
--                    v9 marked scripts as captured BEFORE checking capacity
--                    and silently lost them; v10 checks capacity FIRST and
--                    never marks a rejected item as seen)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Queue = {}

Queue.STATES = { "PENDING", "PROCESSING", "COMPLETED", "FAILED", "REJECTED", "RETRY_PENDING" }
Queue.items = {}
Queue.counts = { PENDING = 0, PROCESSING = 0, COMPLETED = 0, FAILED = 0, REJECTED = 0, RETRY_PENDING = 0 }
Queue.CAP = { script = Core.CFG.MAX_SCRIPT_QUEUE, gui = Core.CFG.MAX_GUI_QUEUE, dynsrc = Core.CFG.MAX_SCRIPT_QUEUE }
Queue.HISTORY_CAP = 2000      -- terminal tickets kept for the report (rotating)
Queue.rejectedSamples = {}    -- last reasons (for Missing Data)
Queue._qid = 0
Queue._running = false

local function qCount(state)
    return Queue.counts[state] or 0
end

local function qActive()
