        table.remove(HttpBlock.last, 1)
    end
    pcall(function()
        Rec.record(Rec.buckets.HTTPBLK, "httpblk", tostring(url),
            "[BLOCKED] " .. tostring(url), nil)
    end)
    Store.markDirty()
end

function HttpBlock.notePassed(url)
    HttpBlock.passed += 1
end

function HttpBlock.setEnabled(on)
    on = on == true
    if on == HttpBlock.enabled then return HttpBlock.enabled end
    if on then
        -- نفس شبكة خطافات Http Spy — تُركّب مرة واحدة (حارس داخلي)
        local ok = HttpSpy.install()
        if not ok then
            Rec.tlSys("مضاد HTTPS: تعذر تثبيت الخطافات (لا hookfunction في هذا المنفّذ) — بقي مطفيًا")
            return false
        end
        HttpBlock.enabled = true
        Core.FEATURES.httpBlock = true
        Rec.tlSys("مضاد HTTPS ON — أي رابط http/https/ws يُحجب فورًا (رد فاشل فارغ) فلا يُنفَّذ الكود الذي بداخله — Http Spy يظل يلتقط كل محجوب بعلامة [BLOCKED]")
    else
        HttpBlock.enabled = false
        Core.FEATURES.httpBlock = false
        Rec.tlSys("مضاد HTTPS OFF — الروابط تعود للمرور الطبيعي (حُجب هذا Session: " .. HttpBlock.blocked .. ")")
    end
    pcall(function() if UI and UI.refresh then UI.refresh() end end)
    return HttpBlock.enabled
end

function HttpBlock.toggle()
    return HttpBlock.setEnabled(not HttpBlock.enabled)
end

function HttpBlock.clear()
    HttpBlock.blocked = 0
    HttpBlock.passed = 0
    HttpBlock.last = {}
    pcall(function() if UI and UI.refresh then UI.refresh() end end)
end

function HttpBlock.stats()
    local allowCount = 0
    for _ in pairs(HttpBlock._allow) do allowCount += 1 end
    return {
        enabled = HttpBlock.enabled,
        logBlocked = HttpBlock.logBlocked,
        blocked = HttpBlock.blocked,
        passed = HttpBlock.passed,
        allowCount = allowCount,
        installed = HttpSpy ~= nil and HttpSpy._installed or false,
    }
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M16 — CONTROL CENTER UI
--  Draggable ScreenGui (gethui() when available). ⑥ v10.1 tabs: Dashboard,
--  Capture, Queue (سرعة/إلغاء), Scripts, GUI, Buttons (مراقبة الأزرار),
--  Timeline, HTTP (Http Spy — مقفل افتراضيًا), Storage, Report, Errors,
--  Caps. Toggles for every
--  feature. Buttons: Start / Pause / Resume / Save / AutoSave / Clear /
--  Copy Report / Snapshot / Add Marker. A "Report Output" TextBox fills
--  PROGRESSIVELY (never freezes the UI; display is a tail view — the full
--  report lives in the chunk store / file).
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
UI = {}

UI.root = nil
UI.visible = true
UI._light = false
UI._built = false
UI._reportBuilding = false
UI._clearArmed = 0
UI._cancelArmed = 0     -- ⑥ تأكيد إلغاء الطابور بخطوتين
UI.refs = {}          -- widget references for refresh

-- COSMIC UI THEME — smoked glass, pearl text, and restrained ice-blue accents.
-- Theme keys are kept compatible with the original controls and refresh code.
local THEME = {
    bg          = Color3.fromRGB(3, 7, 15),
    main        = Color3.fromRGB(7, 13, 24),
    panel       = Color3.fromRGB(12, 25, 43),
    panel2      = Color3.fromRGB(22, 43, 68),
    stroke      = Color3.fromRGB(190, 219, 246),
    text        = Color3.fromRGB(250, 252, 255),
    dim         = Color3.fromRGB(211, 225, 241),
    green       = Color3.fromRGB(226, 241, 255), -- legacy key: pearl / ice silver
    greenD      = Color3.fromRGB(31, 91, 139),
    red         = Color3.fromRGB(201, 83, 110),
    redD        = Color3.fromRGB(91, 39, 57),
    blue        = Color3.fromRGB(126, 204, 255),
    buttonHover = Color3.fromRGB(52, 91, 132),
    active      = Color3.fromRGB(30, 78, 119),
}
local UI_FONT   = Enum.Font.SourceSans          -- يدعم تشكيل العربية
local UI_FONT_B = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
-- ⑥ إصلاح v10.1: UDim2new كانت مستخدمة في كل الواجهة بلا تعريف —
-- الواجهة كانت ستفشل بصمت (pcall) ويدخل السكربت وضع بلا واجهة!
local UDim2new = UDim2.new
local function mk(class, props, parent)
    local o = Instance.new(class)
    if props then
        for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
    end
    if parent then o.Parent = parent end
    return o
end

-- Motion preference: respect Roblox's Reduce Motion accessibility setting.
local UI_REDUCED_MOTION = false
pcall(function()
    UI_REDUCED_MOTION = game:GetService("GuiService").ReducedMotionEnabled == true
end)
local function uiTween(object, info, goals)
    if UI_REDUCED_MOTION then
        info = TweenInfo.new(0)
    end
    local tween = TweenService:Create(object, info, goals)
    tween:Play()
    return tween
end
pcall(function()
    local guiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
    guiService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ReducedMotionEnabled"):Connect(function()
        UI_REDUCED_MOTION = guiService.ReducedMotionEnabled == true
    end)
end)

-- Tactile feedback: a restrained hover lift and a spring-like press/release.
local function addPressEffect(button)
    local scale = mk("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, button)
    local hovered = false
    local activeTween
    local function animateScale(target, duration, style, direction)
        if not scale.Parent then return end
        if activeTween then pcall(function() activeTween:Cancel() end) end
        activeTween = uiTween(scale,
            TweenInfo.new(duration, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
                direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Scale = target })
    end
    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        hovered = true
        animateScale(1.025, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        hovered = false
        animateScale(1, 0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    end)
    button.Activated:Connect(function()
        if not scale.Parent then return end
        if UI_REDUCED_MOTION then
            scale.Scale = 1
            return
        end
        animateScale(0.955, 0.065, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
        if activeTween then
            activeTween.Completed:Connect(function(state)
                if state == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed and scale.Parent then
                    animateScale(hovered and 1.025 or 1, 0.19,
                        Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function mkBtn(text, layoutOrder, parent, onClick, w, h)
    local b = mk("TextButton", {
        Name = text,
        Text = text,
        Size = UDim2new(0, w or 96, 0, h or 24),
        BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.62,
        TextColor3 = THEME.text,
        TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.20,
        Font = UI_FONT_B,
        TextSize = 12,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        LayoutOrder = layoutOrder,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
    }, parent)
    mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, b)
    local buttonStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.58 }, b)
    addPressEffect(b)
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            BackgroundColor3 = THEME.buttonHover,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.43,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(buttonStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { Transparency = 0.24 }):Play()
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.62,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(buttonStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Transparency = 0.58 }):Play()
    end)
    b.Activated:Connect(function()
        pcall(onClick, b)
    end)
    return b
end

local function mkLabel(text, size, order, parent, color)
    return mk("TextLabel", {
        Name = "lbl_" .. tostring(order),
        Text = text,
        Size = size or UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        TextColor3 = color or THEME.text,
        TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.20,
        Font = UI_FONT,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextWrapped = true,
        LayoutOrder = order,
    }, parent)
end

local function mkToggle(parent, order, label, getFn, setFn)
    local holder = mk("Frame", {
        Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        LayoutOrder = order,
    }, parent)
    local name = mkLabel(label, UDim2new(1, -88, 1, 0), 1, holder)
    name.Position = UDim2new(0, 4, 0, 0)
    local btn = mk("TextButton", {
        Text = "...",
        Size = UDim2new(0, 64, 0, 20),
        Position = UDim2new(1, -70, 0, 2),
        BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.56,
        TextColor3 = THEME.text,
        TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.20,
        Font = UI_FONT_B,
        TextSize = 12,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
    }, holder)
    mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, btn)
    local toggleStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.48 }, btn)
    addPressEffect(btn)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.43,
        }):Play()
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.56,
        }):Play()
    end)
    local function paint()
        local on = getFn() == true
        btn.Text = on and "ON" or "OFF"
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and THEME.greenD or THEME.redD
        toggleStroke.Color = on and THEME.blue or THEME.stroke
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.36 or 0.56
        toggleStroke.Transparency = on and 0.24 or 0.58
    end
    btn.Activated:Connect(function()
        setFn(not getFn())
        paint()
        pcall(UI.refresh)
    end)
    paint()
    return { button = btn, paint = paint, label = name }
end

function UI.setLightMode(on)
    UI._light = on == true
    if UI.refs.statusLight then
        UI.refs.statusLight.Text = UI._light and "خفيف" or "عادي"
        UI.refs.statusLight.TextColor3 = UI._light and Color3.fromRGB(255, 199, 116) or THEME.green
    end
end

local function buildDashboard(content)
    local list = mk("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 3),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, content)
    UI.refs.dashLines = {}
    local labels = {
        "state", "events", "scripts", "guis", "funcs", "remotes",
        "timeline", "errors", "quality", "save",
    }
    for i, key in ipairs(labels) do
        local l = mkLabel("…", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 14), i, content)
        l.TextColor3 = THEME.text
        UI.refs.dashLines[key] = l
    end

    -- ③ زر التشغيل الرئيسي — التسجيل لا يبدأ إلا من هنا
    local masterRow = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = #labels + 1 }, content)
    local masterBtn = mkBtn("▶ تشغيل التسجيل", 1, masterRow, function()
        Boot.enableCapture(not Core.CAPTURING)
        pcall(UI.refresh)
    end, 1, 0)
    masterBtn.Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 26)
    masterBtn.BackgroundColor3 = THEME.red
    masterBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.24
    UI.refs.masterBtn = masterBtn

    local row = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = #labels + 2 }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, row)
    mkBtn("تشغيل", 1, row, function() Rec.controls.start(); UI.refresh() end, 68)
    mkBtn("إيقاف مؤقت", 2, row, function() Rec.controls.pause(); UI.refresh() end, 68)
    mkBtn("استئناف", 3, row, function() Rec.controls.resume(); UI.refresh() end, 68)
    mkBtn("إيقاف", 4, row, function() Rec.controls.stop(); UI.refresh() end, 68)
    local row2 = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = #labels + 3 }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, row2)
    mkBtn("لقطة بيئة", 1, row2, function() Snap.take("ui") end, 68)
    mkBtn("مؤشر", 2, row2, function() Rec.controls.marker("ui-marker") end, 68)
    mkBtn("اختبار ذاتي", 3, row2, function() task.spawn(function() Tests.run() end) end, 68)
    mkBtn("إعادة فحص", 4, row2, function() Boot.rescanAll() end, 68)
    return list
end

        return Core.FEATURES.scriptDumper
    end, function(v) Core.FEATURES.scriptDumper = v end)

        return Core.FEATURES.guiCapture
    end, function(v) Core.FEATURES.guiCapture = v end)

    mkToggle(content, i, "كتابة الخصائص (__newindex)", function()
        return Core.FEATURES.propWrites
    end, function(v) Core.FEATURES.propWrites = v end)
    i += 1

        return Core.FEATURES.namecallSlow
    end, function(v) Core.FEATURES.namecallSlow = v end)
    i += 1

        return Core.FEATURES.incoming
    end, function(v) Core.FEATURES.incoming = v end)

    mkToggle(content, i, "فحص الذاكرة (دوال gc)", function()
        return Core.FEATURES.memoryScan
    end, function(v) Core.FEATURES.memoryScan = v end)

    mkLabel("الوضع الخفيف يشتغل تلقائيًا تحت " .. Core.CFG.LIGHT_FPS_ON

local function buildStorage(content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)
    UI.refs.storeLines = {}
    for idx = 1, 8 do
        local l = mkLabel("…", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 13), idx, content)

    end
    local row = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), LayoutOrder = 20, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,

        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, row)
    mkBtn("احفظ الآن", 1, row, function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, err = Store.save("manual-ui")
            UI.refresh()
            Rec.tlSys(ok and "حفظ يدوي ناجح" or ("فشل الحفظ اليدوي: " .. tostring(err)))
        end)
    end, 78)
    UI.refs.autoSaveBtn = mkBtn("تلقائي: OFF", 2, row, function(b)
        Store.setAutoSave(not Store.autosave, "user-ui")
        b.Text = Store.autosave and "تلقائي: ON" or "تلقائي: OFF"
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Store.autosave and THEME.greenD or THEME.redD
        UI.refresh()
    end, 78)
    UI.refs.autoSaveBtn.BackgroundColor3 = THEME.redD
    local row2 = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), LayoutOrder = 21, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, row2)
    mkBtn("تفريغ الذاكرة", 1, row2, function(b)
        -- تأكيد بخطوتين؛ لا يُحذف أي ملف على القرص أبدًا
        if os.clock() - UI._clearArmed < 4 then
            Boot.clearMemory()
            b.Text = "تفريغ الذاكرة"
            UI._clearArmed = 0
        else
            UI._clearArmed = os.clock()
            b.Text = "تأكيد التفريغ؟"
            task.delay(4.2, function()
                if os.clock() - UI._clearArmed >= 4 then
                    b.Text = "تفريغ الذاكرة"
                end
            end)
        end
    end, 78)
    mkLabel("التفريغ يمسح ذاكرة الجلسة فقط — ملف " .. Core.FILE_REPORT .. " لا يُحذف أبدًا. الحفظ التلقائي مغلق عند الإقلاع.", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 26), 30, content, THEME.dim)
end

local function buildReport(content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)

        end)

    end, 72)
    mkBtn("نسخ الكامل", 4, row, function()

            if okBuild and report then
                local ok = Caps.copy(report)
                Rec.tlSys(ok and ("تم نسخ التقرير الكامل (" .. #report .. " بايت)") or "فشل النسخ")
            end
        end)
    end, 72)
    local status = mkLabel("(خامل — اضغط بناء التقرير؛ يملأ تدريجيًا)", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 13), 2, content, THEME.dim)
    UI.refs.reportStatus = status

        Size = UDim2new(1, -10, 1, -46),
        BackgroundColor3 = THEME.bg,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.42,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,

        ScrollBarThickness = 5,
        LayoutOrder = 3,
    }, content)
    mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, boxHolder)
    mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.72 }, boxHolder)
    local box = mk("TextBox", {
        Name = "Report Output",
        Text = "",
        Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 20),
        Position = UDim2new(0, 4, 0, 4),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 195),
        Font = UI_FONT,
        TextSize = 10,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
        TextWrapped = false,
        MultiLine = true,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        TextEditable = false,
        Active = true,
    }, boxHolder)
    UI.refs.reportBox = box
    UI.refs.reportHolder = boxHolder
end

-- ═══════════ ⑥ v10.1 — تبويب الطابور (سرعة / إيقاف مؤقت / إلغاء) ═══════════
local function buildQueue(content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)
    UI.refs.queueLines = {}
    for idx = 1, 5 do
        local l = mkLabel("…", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 13), idx, content)
        l.TextColor3 = THEME.text
        UI.refs.queueLines[idx] = l
    end
    local row1 = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 10 }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, row1)
    mkBtn("أبطأ", 1, row1, function() Queue.speedPreset("slow") end, 56)
    mkBtn("عادي", 2, row1, function() Queue.speedPreset("normal") end, 56)
    mkBtn("سريع", 3, row1, function() Queue.speedPreset("fast") end, 56)
    mkBtn("أقصى", 4, row1, function() Queue.speedPreset("turbo") end, 56)
    local row2 = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 11 }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, row2)
    mkBtn("−", 1, row2, function() Queue.setSpeed(Queue.interval * 1.5, Queue.batch) end, 34)
    mkBtn("+", 2, row2, function() Queue.setSpeed(Queue.interval / 1.5, Queue.batch) end, 34)
    mkBtn("⏸ إيقاف مؤقت", 3, row2, function(b)
        if Queue.paused then
            Queue.resume()
            b.Text = "⏸ إيقاف مؤقت"
        else
            Queue.pause()
            b.Text = "▶ استئناف الطابور"
        end
    end, 88)
    mkBtn("✖ إلغاء الطابور", 4, row2, function(b)
        -- ⑥ تأكيد بخطوتين: لا إلغاء بضغطة واحدة بالخطأ
        if os.clock() - UI._cancelArmed < 4 then
            Queue.cancelPending("ui")
            b.Text = "✖ إلغاء الطابور"
            UI._cancelArmed = 0
        else
            UI._cancelArmed = os.clock()
            b.Text = "تأكيد الإلغاء؟"
            task.delay(4.2, function()
                if os.clock() - UI._cancelArmed >= 4 then
                    b.Text = "✖ إلغاء الطابور"
                end
            end)
        end
    end, 96)
    mkLabel("السرعة = فاصل الخطوات × الدفعة. الإلغاء يسقط العناصر المنتظرة فقط — العنصر قيد المعالجة يكتمل والتاريخ المكتمل/الفاشل لا يُمس.", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 26), 30, content, THEME.dim)
end

-- ═══════════ ⑦ v10.2 — تبويب HTTP (Http Spy بزر تشغيل/إطفاء — طافي OFF عند الفتح) ═══════════
local function buildHttp(content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)
    local i = 0
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "Http Spy (زر ON/OFF)", function()
        return HttpSpy.enabled
    end, function(v) HttpSpy.setEnabled(v) end)
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "إظهار الردود (Status/Body)", function()
        return HttpSpy.showResponses
    end, function(v) HttpSpy.showResponses = v end)
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "فك JSON تلقائيًا", function()
        return HttpSpy.autoDecode
    end, function(v) HttpSpy.autoDecode = v end)
    i += 1
    local row = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, row)
    mkBtn("تفريغ السجل", 1, row, function() HttpSpy.clear() end, 84)
    mkBtn("نسخ آخر رابط", 2, row, function()
        local last
        for idx = #HttpSpy.logs, 1, -1 do
            if HttpSpy.logs[idx].url then last = HttpSpy.logs[idx].url break end
        end
        if last then
            local ok = Caps.copy(last)
            Rec.tlSys(ok and ("HTTP COPY: " .. Core.preview(last, 120)) or "فشل النسخ (لا setclipboard؟)")
        else
            Rec.tlSys("HTTP COPY: لا رابط بعد")
        end
    end, 96)
    UI.refs.httpLines = {}
    for idx = 1, 9 do
        local l = mkLabel("…", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 13), 100 + idx, content)
        l.TextColor3 = THEME.text
        l.TextSize = 10
        UI.refs.httpLines[idx] = l
    end
    mkLabel("v10.3 — نفس تقنية الأصل + تغطية أشمل: hookfunction(request + http_request + syn.request) + replaceclosure + __namecall (HttpGet/HttpPost/GetObjects/HttpService) + http.get/http.post + WebSocket — يلقط pastefy/pastebin/github/raw.githubusercontent مع الرد. ملاحظة: فعّله قبل تشغيل السكربت الذي تريد مراقبته. عرض فقط — الحجب من تبويب (مضاد HTTPS).", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 42), 200, content, THEME.dim)
end


-- ═══════════ ⑧ v10.3 — تبويب مضاد HTTPS (حجب الروابط) ═══════════
local function buildBlock(content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)
    local i = 0
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "مضاد HTTPS (حجب كل الروابط)", function()
        return HttpBlock.enabled
    end, function(v) HttpBlock.setEnabled(v) end)
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "تسجيل المحجوب في تبويب HTTP", function()
        return HttpBlock.logBlocked
    end, function(v) HttpBlock.logBlocked = v end)
    i += 1
    -- صف قائمة السماح: مربع نص + زر اسمح + زر امنع الكل (مواضع صريحة — لا تراكب)
    local row = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i }, content)
    local tb = mk("TextBox", {
        Text = "",
        Size = UDim2new(1, -146, 0, 20),
        BackgroundColor3 = THEME.bg,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.35,
        TextColor3 = THEME.text,
        TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
        PlaceholderText = "رابط/بادئة مسموحة (اختياري)…",
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        Font = UI_FONT,
        TextSize = 10,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }, row)
    mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, tb)
    mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.72 }, tb)
    local allowBtn = mkBtn("اسمح", 1, row, function()
        if #tb.Text > 0 then
            HttpBlock.allow(tb.Text)
            tb.Text = ""
            UI.refresh()
        end
    end, 62)
    allowBtn.Position = UDim2new(1, -142, 0, 0)
    local denyBtn = mkBtn("امنع الكل", 2, row, function()
        HttpBlock.clearAllow()
        UI.refresh()
    end, 72)
    denyBtn.Position = UDim2new(1, -76, 0, 0)
    i += 1
    UI.refs.blkLines = {}
    for idx = 1, 8 do
        local l = mkLabel("…", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 13), 100 + idx, content)
        l.TextColor3 = THEME.text
        l.TextSize = 10
        UI.refs.blkLines[idx] = l
    end
    mkLabel("عند التفعيل: أي رابط http/https/ws/wss يُحجب قبل إرساله — يرجع للسكربت رد فاشل فارغ (Success=false و403 وBody فارغ) فلا يُنفَّذ الكود الذي بداخله أبدًا. Http Spy يظل يلتقط كل رابط محجوب بعلامة [BLOCKED] في تبويب HTTP حتى لو كان زره مطفيًا. روابط rbxassetid الداخلية للعبة لا تُحجب.", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 42), 200, content, THEME.dim)
end

-- ═══════════ ⑥ v10.1 — تبويب الأزرار (مراقبة الأزرار) ═══════════
local function buildBtn(content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, content)
    local i = 0
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "مراقبة الأزرار", function()
        return BMon.enabled
    end, function(v) BMon.setEnabled(v) end)
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "أزرار النظام/المنفّذ أيضًا", function()
        return BMon.includeNoise
    end, function(v)
        BMon.includeNoise = v
        if BMon.enabled then task.spawn(function() pcall(BMon._sweep) end) end
    end)
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "الواجهات الطافية (nil)", function()
        return BMon.includeNil
    end, function(v) BMon.includeNil = v end)
    i += 1
    mkToggle(content, i, "تشغيل التسجيل تلقائيًا عند الضغط", function()
        return BMon.autoRecord
    end, function(v) BMon.autoRecord = v end)
    i += 1
    local row = mk("Frame", { Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, 24), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i }, content)
    mk("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, row)
    local winLbl = mkLabel("النافذة: …", UDim2new(0, 92, 1, 0), 1, row)
    winLbl.LayoutOrder = 1
    UI.refs.btnWinLbl = winLbl
    mkBtn("−", 2, row, function()
        BMon.window = math.clamp(BMon.window - 1, 2, 10)
        BMon.assocWindow = BMon.window
        UI.refresh()
    end, 34)
    mkBtn("+", 3, row, function()
        BMon.window = math.clamp(BMon.window + 1, 2, 10)
        BMon.assocWindow = BMon.window
        UI.refresh()
    end, 34)
    mkBtn("تفريغ الضغطات", 4, row, function() BMon.clear() end, 100)
    UI.refs.btnLines = {}
    for idx = 1, 9 do
        local l = mkLabel("…", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 13), 100 + idx, content)
        l.TextColor3 = THEME.text
        l.TextSize = 10
        UI.refs.btnLines[idx] = l
    end
    mkLabel("زر الالتقاط: طافي (OFF) عند أول فتح — فعّله بزر (مراقبة الأزرار) مثل Http Spy. فور تفعيله: كل ضغطة زر تُلتقط بكل قوتها (يبدأ التسجيل تلقائيًا إن كان مطفأً) مع أول رابط HTTP لها.", UDim2new(1, -12, 0, 26), 200, content, THEME.dim)
end


-- ---- main build -------------------------------------------------------------
function UI.build()
    if UI._built then return end
    local okAll, err = pcall(function()
        Core.internal(function()
            local parent
            if Caps.has("gethui") then
                local ok, h = pcall(gethui)
                if ok and h then parent = h end
            end
            if not parent then
                local lp = Core.localPlayer()
                parent = lp and lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5) or nil
            end
            if not parent then error("no UI parent (gethui/PlayerGui unavailable)") end

            -- Remove only older copies of TRO's own ScreenGui. Re-running the
            -- script must not leave an older dim/overlay layer stacked underneath.
            local function removePreviousTROGui(container)
                if not container then return end
                pcall(function()
                    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == Core.GUI_ROOT_NAME and child:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                            child:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            removePreviousTROGui(parent)
            local cleanupPlayer = Core.localPlayer()
            if cleanupPlayer then
                local cleanupGui = cleanupPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                if cleanupGui and cleanupGui ~= parent then
                    removePreviousTROGui(cleanupGui)
                end
            end

            local gui = mk("ScreenGui", {
                Name = Core.GUI_ROOT_NAME,
                ResetOnSpawn = false,
                DisplayOrder = 999999,
                IgnoreGuiInset = false,
                ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
            }, parent)
            UI.root = gui
            -- This control center intentionally creates no full-screen dimmer,
            -- BlurEffect, or Lighting color-correction layer; effects stay inside Main.

            -- The supplied art is panoramic (1536:683). Keep its aspect ratio
            -- while the user resizes from the lower-right grip.
            local WINDOW_ASPECT = 1536 / 683
            local windowW, windowH = 720, 320
            local MIN_WINDOW_W, MAX_WINDOW_W = 600, 1120
            local main = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "Main",
                Size = UDim2new(0, windowW, 0, windowH),
                Position = UDim2new(0.5, -windowW / 2, 0.5, -windowH / 2),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.main,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Active = true,
                ClipsDescendants = true,
            }, gui)
            UI.refs.main = main
            local mainScale = mk("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, main)
            -- Separate entrance scale keeps responsive sizing independent from animation.
            local introScale = mk("UIScale", { Scale = UI_REDUCED_MOTION and 1 or 0.93 }, main)
            UI.refs.introScale = introScale
            local function updateMainScale()
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(720, 320)
                local safeW = math.max(260, viewport.X - 24)
                local safeH = math.max(160, viewport.Y - 36)
                local fit = math.min(safeW / windowW, safeH / windowH, 1)
                mainScale.Scale = math.clamp(fit, 0.36, 1)
            end
            updateMainScale()
            pcall(function()
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if camera then
                    camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateMainScale)
                end
                workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
                    updateMainScale()
                    local current = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    if current then
                        current:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateMainScale)
                    end
                end)
            end)

            -- Full-strength artwork: no black sheet over the image.
            -- The glass surfaces below are localized so the nebula stays visible.
            local bgImage = mk("ImageLabel", {
                Name = "CosmicBackground",
                Size = UDim2new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Image = "rbxassetid://132688312189385",
                ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                ImageTransparency = 0,
                ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 1,
            }, main)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13) }, bgImage)

            -- Premium shell: a crisp chrome rim and a slow, low-intensity light pulse.
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13) }, main)
            local mainStroke = mk("UIStroke", {
                Color = THEME.stroke,
                Thickness = 1.6,
                Transparency = 0.22,
            }, main)
            local innerRim = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "InnerRim",
                Size = UDim2new(1, -4, 1, -4),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 2, 0, 2),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Active = false,
            }, main)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11) }, innerRim)
            mk("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.fromRGB(238, 247, 255), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.88 }, innerRim)
            pcall(function()
                TweenService:Create(
                    mainStroke,
                    TweenInfo.new(4.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
                    { Transparency = 0.44 }
                ):Play()
            end)

            -- Header is a narrow smoked-glass strip, not a full-screen dimmer.
            local bar = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "TitleBar",
                Size = UDim2new(1, 0, 0, 36),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.bg,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.82,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, main)
            UI.refs.titleBar = bar
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13) }, bar)
            mk("UIGradient", {
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(3, 9, 20)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB(14, 34, 57)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(4, 10, 22)),
                }),
                Rotation = 0,
            }, bar)
            local titleMark = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "TitleMark",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 3, 0, 20),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 14, 0, 8),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.blue,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, bar)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, titleMark)
            mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.blue, Thickness = 2, Transparency = 0.68 }, titleMark)
            mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = "TRO v" .. Core.VERSION .. "  /  COSMIC COMMAND",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 340, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 25, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.green,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 14,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.16,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            }, bar)

            -- Performance readout sits in its own compact glass capsule.
            local statPill = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "PerformanceCapsule",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 148, 0, 22),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -258, 0, 7),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.42,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, bar)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, statPill)
            mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.62 }, statPill)
            local stat = mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = "…",
                Size = UDim2new(1, -12, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 6, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.green,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            }, statPill)
            UI.refs.perfLabel = stat
            local lightPill = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "LightModeCapsule",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 44, 0, 22),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -108, 0, 7),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.48,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, bar)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, lightPill)
            mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.68 }, lightPill)
            local lightL = mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = "عادي",
                Size = UDim2new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.green,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
            }, lightPill)
            UI.refs.statusLight = lightL

            local closeBtn = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = "×",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 22, 0, 22),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -29, 0, 7),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.redD,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.28,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 244, 247),
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 17,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
            }, bar)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, closeBtn)
            local closeStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.red, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.38 }, closeBtn)
            addPressEffect(closeBtn)
            closeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(closeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.13), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = THEME.red, BackgroundTransparency = 0.10,
                }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(closeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.13), { Transparency = 0.12 }):Play()
            end)
            closeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                TweenService:Create(closeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.17), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = THEME.redD, BackgroundTransparency = 0.28,
                }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(closeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.17), { Transparency = 0.38 }):Play()
            end)
            closeBtn.Activated:Connect(function() UI.hide() end)

            -- Minimize into a floating recovery chip (the close button still fully hides UI).
            local restoreDock
            local minimizeBtn = mk("TextButton", {
                Name = "MinimizeButton",
                Text = "−",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 22, 0, 22),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -55, 0, 7),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.32,
                TextColor3 = THEME.text,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 17,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
            }, bar)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, minimizeBtn)
            local minimizeStroke = mk("UIStroke", {
                Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.46,
            }, minimizeBtn)
            addPressEffect(minimizeBtn)
            minimizeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                uiTween(minimizeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.13), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = THEME.blue, BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
                })
                uiTween(minimizeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.13), { Transparency = 0.12 })
            end)
            minimizeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                uiTween(minimizeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.17), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2, BackgroundTransparency = 0.32,
                })
                uiTween(minimizeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.17), { Transparency = 0.46 })
            end)
            minimizeBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
                main.Visible = false
                UI.visible = false
                if restoreDock then restoreDock.Visible = true end
            end)

            -- A compact live marker adds status without competing with the artwork.
            local liveDot = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "LiveIndicator",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 7, 0, 7),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 386, 0, 14),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(124, 218, 255),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, bar)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, liveDot)
            local liveGlow = mk("UIStroke", {
                Color = THEME.blue, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.24,
            }, liveDot)
            pcall(function()
                uiTween(liveGlow,
                    TweenInfo.new(1.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
                    { Transparency = 0.78, Thickness = 3 })
            end)
            mk("TextLabel", {
                Name = "LiveText",
                Text = "LIVE / LOCAL",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 62, 0, 16),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 398, 0, 10),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.dim,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.24,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 8,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            }, bar)

            -- A small moving highlight gives the header a polished glass edge.
            local topSweep = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "HeaderLightSweep",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 100, 0, 1),
                Position = UDim2new(0, -110, 1, -1),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.blue,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.18,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, bar)
            mk("UIGradient", {
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                }),
            }, topSweep)
            pcall(function()
                TweenService:Create(
                    topSweep,
                    TweenInfo.new(5.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, false),
                    { Position = UDim2new(1, 10, 1, -1) }
                ):Play()
            end)

            -- Floating rescue chip is fixed to the screen while the main window may
            -- travel freely beyond every screen edge. It also restores a minimized UI.
            restoreDock = mk("TextButton", {
                Name = "TRO_RestoreDock",
                Text = "TRO  ↗",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 84, 0, 38),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -16, 1, -18),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel2,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.16,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 12,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Visible = false,
                ZIndex = 100,
            }, gui)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11) }, restoreDock)
            local dockStroke = mk("UIStroke", {
                Color = THEME.blue, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.18,
            }, restoreDock)
            mk("UIGradient", {
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(24, 57, 88)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(47, 88, 125)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(12, 31, 53)),
                }),
                Rotation = 0,
            }, restoreDock)
            addPressEffect(restoreDock)
            UI.refs.restoreDock = restoreDock

            local function updateOffscreenDock()
                if not restoreDock or not main.Parent then return end
                if not main.Visible then
                    restoreDock.Visible = true
                    return
                end
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(720, 320)
                local hp, hs = bar.AbsolutePosition, bar.AbsoluteSize
                -- Show rescue as soon as the draggable header is no longer reachable.
                local headerOutside = (hp.X + hs.X < 2) or (hp.X > viewport.X - 2)
                    or (hp.Y + hs.Y < 2) or (hp.Y > viewport.Y - 2)
                restoreDock.Visible = headerOutside
            end

            local function restoreToCenter()
                main.Visible = true
                UI.visible = true
                restoreDock.Visible = false
                uiTween(main, TweenInfo.new(0.46, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    Position = UDim2new(0.5, -windowW / 2, 0.5, -windowH / 2),
                })
                if introScale then
                    introScale.Scale = UI_REDUCED_MOTION and 1 or 0.965
                    uiTween(introScale,
                        TweenInfo.new(0.38, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                        { Scale = 1 })
                end
                task.delay(0.5, function()
                    if UI.visible then pcall(UI.refresh) end
                    updateOffscreenDock()
                end)
            end
            restoreDock.Activated:Connect(restoreToCenter)
            restoreDock.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                uiTween(restoreDock, TweenInfo.new(0.14), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.02,
                })
                uiTween(dockStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { Transparency = 0.02, Thickness = 2 })
            end)
            restoreDock.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                uiTween(restoreDock, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
                })
                uiTween(dockStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Transparency = 0.18, Thickness = 1.5 })
            end)

            -- Free drag on mouse and touch. No viewport clamp: the user can move
            -- the panel completely off-screen; the pinned TRO chip brings it back.
            do
                local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
                local dragging, dragStart, startPos, dragInput = false, nil, nil, nil
                bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        local localX = input.Position.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X
                        if localX > 445 then return end
                        dragging = true
                        dragStart = input.Position
                        startPos = main.Position
                        dragInput = input
                    end
                end)
                UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                        or input == dragInput) then
                        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                        local nx = startPos.X.Offset + delta.X
                        local ny = startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                        main.Position = UDim2new(0.5, nx, 0.5, ny)
                        updateOffscreenDock()
                    end
                end)
                UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input == dragInput then
                        dragging = false
                        dragInput = nil
                        updateOffscreenDock()
                    end
                end)
                main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(updateOffscreenDock)
                bar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"):Connect(updateOffscreenDock)
            end

            -- Optional keyboard recovery: RightShift recenters the panel.
            pcall(function()
                game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
                    if not processed and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift then
                        if not main.Visible or restoreDock.Visible then
                            restoreToCenter()
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end)

            -- Compact glass sidebar. The buttons scroll instead of being squeezed.
            local nav = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
                Name = "Navigation",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 126, 1, -54),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 0, 0, 36),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.bg,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.89,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                CanvasSize = UDim2new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
                ScrollBarThickness = 2,
                ScrollBarImageColor3 = THEME.blue,
                ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.22,
            }, main)
            mk("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, nav)
            mk("UIPadding", {
                PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
                PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7),
            }, nav)
            local navDivider = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "NavDivider",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 1, 1, -76),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 126, 0, 44),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.stroke,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.62,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, main)
            mk("UIGradient", {
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.18, 0.1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.82, 0.1),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                }),
                Rotation = 90,
            }, navDivider)

            -- Main glass panel is deliberately very transparent so the artwork reads through it.
            local contentArea = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "ContentGlass",
                Size = UDim2new(1, -146, 1, -76),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 136, 0, 42),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.main,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.91,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ClipsDescendants = true,
            }, main)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10) }, contentArea)
            local contentGlassStroke = mk("UIStroke", {
                Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.70,
            }, contentArea)
            pcall(function()
                uiTween(contentGlassStroke,
                    TweenInfo.new(3.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
                    { Transparency = 0.44, Color = THEME.blue })
            end)
            local pageTitle = mk("TextLabel", {
                Name = "PageTitle",
                Text = "الرئيسية",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 260, 0, 20),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 12, 0, 6),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.green,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.12,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 14,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            }, contentArea)
            UI.refs.pageTitle = pageTitle
            local pageMeta = mk("TextLabel", {
                Name = "PageMeta",
                Text = "MODULE 01 / 13",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 112, 0, 18),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -124, 0, 7),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.dim,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            }, contentArea)
            UI.refs.pageMeta = pageMeta
            local pageRule = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "PageRule",
                Size = UDim2new(1, -24, 0, 1),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 12, 0, 31),
                BackgroundColor3 = THEME.stroke,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.72,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
            }, contentArea)
            local pageRuleGradient = mk("UIGradient", {
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.72),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0.08),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.08),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.72),
                }),
            }, pageRule)
            pcall(function()
                uiTween(pageRuleGradient,
                    TweenInfo.new(8, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, false),
                    { Rotation = 180 })
            end)

            local tabDefs = {
                { key = "dash",  label = "الرئيسية",   build = buildDashboard },
                { key = "cap",   label = "الالتقاط",   build = buildCapture },
                { key = "q",     label = "الطابور",     build = buildQueue },     -- ⑥ v10.1
                { key = "scr",   label = "السكربتات",  build = buildScripts },
                { key = "gui",   label = "الواجهات",   build = buildGui },
                { key = "btn",   label = "الأزرار",     build = buildBtn },       -- ⑥ v10.1
                { key = "tl",    label = "الزمن",      build = buildTimeline },
                { key = "http",  label = "HTTP",        build = buildHttp },      -- ⑥ v10.1
                { key = "blk",   label = "مضاد HTTPS",  build = buildBlock },     -- ⑧ v10.3
                { key = "store", label = "الحفظ",      build = buildStorage },
                { key = "rep",   label = "التقرير",    build = buildReport },
                { key = "err",   label = "الأخطاء",    build = buildErrors },
                { key = "caps",  label = "القدرات",    build = buildCaps },
            }

            local function selectTab(key)
                for idx, def in ipairs(tabDefs) do
                    if TABS[def.key] then
                        local selected = (def.key == key)
                        TABS[def.key].content.Visible = selected
                        if selected and TABS[def.key].pageScale then
                            TABS[def.key].pageScale.Scale = UI_REDUCED_MOTION and 1 or 0.985
                            uiTween(TABS[def.key].pageScale,
                                TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                                { Scale = 1 })
                        end
                        TABS[def.key].btn.BackgroundColor3 = selected and THEME.active or THEME.panel
                        if TABS[def.key].indicator then
                            TweenService:Create(TABS[def.key].indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                                BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1,
                                Size = selected and UDim2new(0, 3, 0, 13) or UDim2new(0, 3, 0, 9),
                            }):Play()
                        end
                        TABS[def.key].btn.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.32 or 0.68
                        TABS[def.key].btn.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or THEME.text
                        local tabStroke = TABS[def.key].btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                        if tabStroke then
                            tabStroke.Color = selected and THEME.blue or THEME.stroke
                            tabStroke.Transparency = selected and 0.18 or 0.78
                        end
                        if selected then
                            if UI.refs.pageTitle then
                                UI.refs.pageTitle.Text = def.label
                                UI.refs.pageTitle.TextTransparency = UI_REDUCED_MOTION and 0 or 0.20
                                uiTween(UI.refs.pageTitle,
                                    TweenInfo.new(0.20, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                                    { TextTransparency = 0 })
                            end
                            if UI.refs.pageMeta then
                                UI.refs.pageMeta.Text = string.format("MODULE %02d / %02d", idx, #tabDefs)
                                UI.refs.pageMeta.TextTransparency = UI_REDUCED_MOTION and 0 or 0.35
                                uiTween(UI.refs.pageMeta,
                                    TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                                    { TextTransparency = 0 })
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                UI._activeTab = key
            end

            for idx, def in ipairs(tabDefs) do
                local b = mk("TextButton", {
                    Text = def.label,
                    Size = UDim2new(1, 0, 0, 19),
                    BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.68,
                    TextColor3 = THEME.text,
                    TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                    TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
                    Font = UI_FONT_B,
                    TextSize = 11,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    LayoutOrder = idx,
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                }, nav)
                mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, b)
                local tabStroke = mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.stroke, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.78 }, b)
                local indicator = mk("Frame", {
                    Name = "ActiveIndicator",
                    Size = UDim2new(0, 3, 0, 9),
                    Position = UDim2new(1, -5, 0.5, -4.5),
                    BackgroundColor3 = THEME.blue,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                }, b)
                mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, indicator)
                mk("UIStroke", { Color = THEME.blue, Thickness = 2, Transparency = 0.68 }, indicator)
                addPressEffect(b)
                b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    if UI._activeTab ~= def.key then
                        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.14), {
                            BackgroundColor3 = THEME.buttonHover,
                            BackgroundTransparency = 0.48,
                        }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(tabStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.14), {
                            Transparency = 0.38,
                        }):Play()
                    end
                end)
                b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    if UI._activeTab ~= def.key then
                        TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                            BackgroundColor3 = THEME.panel,
                            BackgroundTransparency = 0.68,
                        }):Play()
                        TweenService:Create(tabStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                            Transparency = 0.78,
                        }):Play()
                    end
                end)
                local holder = mk("ScrollingFrame", {
                    Name = "Tab_" .. def.key,
                    Size = UDim2new(1, -10, 1, -44),
                    Position = UDim2new(0, 5, 0, 38),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    CanvasSize = UDim2new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                    AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                    ScrollBarThickness = 3,
                    ScrollBarImageColor3 = THEME.blue,
                    ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.30,
                    Visible = false,
                }, contentArea)
                local pageScale = mk("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, holder)
                mk("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 3), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4) }, holder)
                pcall(def.build, holder)
                TABS[def.key] = { btn = b, content = holder, indicator = indicator, pageScale = pageScale }
                b.Activated:Connect(function()
                    selectTab(def.key)
                end)
            end

            -- footer status line
            local footer = mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = "…",
                Size = UDim2new(1, -154, 0, 13),
                Position = UDim2new(0, 14, 1, -16),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = THEME.dim,
                Font = UI_FONT,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            }, main)
            UI.refs.footer = footer

            -- Live dimensions label, visible only while resizing or hovering.
            local resizeReadout = mk("TextLabel", {
                Name = "ResizeReadout",
                Text = string.format("%d × %d", windowW, windowH),
                Size = UDim2new(0, 76, 0, 15),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -110, 1, -18),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(226, 242, 255),
                TextStrokeColor3 = THEME.bg,
                TextStrokeTransparency = 0.10,
                Font = UI_FONT_B,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                Visible = false,
                ZIndex = 20,
            }, main)
            UI.refs.resizeReadout = resizeReadout

            -- A purpose-built lower-right resize grip. Drag outward to enlarge,
            -- inward to shrink; the artwork's panoramic proportions stay exact.
            local resizeHandle = mk("TextButton", {
                Name = "ResizeHandle",
                Text = "",
                Size = UDim2new(0, 28, 0, 28),
                Position = UDim2new(1, -29, 1, -29),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 46, 72),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.24,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Active = true,
                ZIndex = 21,
            }, main)
            mk("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, resizeHandle)
            local resizeStroke = mk("UIStroke", {
                Color = THEME.blue, Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 0.28,
            }, resizeHandle)
            mk("UIGradient", {
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(46, 100, 148)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(9, 24, 42)),
                }),
                Rotation = 45,
            }, resizeHandle)
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local gripLine = mk("Frame", {
                    Name = "GripLine" .. i,
                    Size = UDim2new(0, 5 + i * 3, 0, 1),
                    Position = UDim2new(1, -6 - (i * 5), 1, -7),
                    Rotation = -45,
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(229, 246, 255),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.12 + (i * 0.12),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    ZIndex = 22,
                }, resizeHandle)
            end
            UI.refs.resizeHandle = resizeHandle

            local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
            local resizing = false
            local resizeStartPointer, resizeStartWidth, resizeStartScale, resizeTouch
            local readoutHideToken = 0
            local function showResizeReadout()
                readoutHideToken += 1
                resizeReadout.Text = string.format("%d × %d", math.floor(windowW + 0.5), math.floor(windowH + 0.5))
                resizeReadout.Visible = true
            end
            local function scheduleReadoutHide()
                readoutHideToken += 1
                local token = readoutHideToken
                task.delay(1.15, function()
                    if token == readoutHideToken and not resizing and resizeReadout.Parent then
                        resizeReadout.Visible = false
                    end
                end)
            end
            resizeHandle.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                showResizeReadout()
                uiTween(resizeHandle, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
                })
                uiTween(resizeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.14), { Transparency = 0.02, Thickness = 1.8 })
            end)
            resizeHandle.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                if not resizing then scheduleReadoutHide() end
                uiTween(resizeHandle, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.24,
                })
                uiTween(resizeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { Transparency = 0.28, Thickness = 1.2 })
            end)
            resizeHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    resizing = true
                    resizeTouch = input
                    resizeStartPointer = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
                    resizeStartWidth = windowW
                    resizeStartScale = math.max(mainScale.Scale * introScale.Scale, 0.36)
                    showResizeReadout()
                    uiTween(resizeHandle, TweenInfo.new(0.10), {
                        BackgroundTransparency = 0.02,
                    })
                    uiTween(resizeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.10), { Transparency = 0, Thickness = 2 })
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if not resizing then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                    or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == resizeTouch) then
                    local dx = (input.Position.X - resizeStartPointer.X) / resizeStartScale
                    local dy = (input.Position.Y - resizeStartPointer.Y) / resizeStartScale
                    -- Pick the dominant pointer axis, then derive the other axis
                    -- from the image ratio so the background never gets distorted.
                    local dyAsWidth = dy * WINDOW_ASPECT
                    local deltaWidth = math.abs(dx) >= math.abs(dyAsWidth) and dx or dyAsWidth
                    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
                    local maxByScreen = math.max(MIN_WINDOW_W, (math.max(viewport.X, viewport.Y) - 32) / 0.55)
                    local effectiveMax = math.min(MAX_WINDOW_W, maxByScreen)
                    windowW = math.clamp(resizeStartWidth + deltaWidth, MIN_WINDOW_W, effectiveMax)
                    windowH = windowW / WINDOW_ASPECT
                    main.Size = UDim2new(0, windowW, 0, windowH)
                    updateMainScale()
                    showResizeReadout()
                    if updateOffscreenDock then updateOffscreenDock() end
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if resizing and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input == resizeTouch) then
                    resizing = false
                    resizeTouch = nil
                    scheduleReadoutHide()
                    uiTween(resizeHandle, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                        BackgroundTransparency = 0.24,
                    })
                    uiTween(resizeStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.16), { Transparency = 0.28, Thickness = 1.2 })
                    if updateOffscreenDock then updateOffscreenDock() end
                end
            end)

            selectTab("dash")

            -- First-open reveal: artwork stays full-strength after the short fade.
            task.defer(function()
                if not main.Parent then return end
                if not UI_REDUCED_MOTION then
                    uiTween(introScale,
                        TweenInfo.new(0.48, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                        { Scale = 1 })
                    -- The supplied artwork is already at full opacity; animate
                    -- only the shell, never fade/dim the image itself.
                else
                    introScale.Scale = 1
                    bgImage.ImageTransparency = 0
                end
                task.delay(0.15, updateOffscreenDock)
            end)
        end)
    end)

    if okAll then
        UI._built = true
        UI.show()
        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                task.wait(UI._light and Core.CFG.UI_REFRESH_LIGHT or Core.CFG.UI_REFRESH_S)
                pcall(UI.refresh)
            end
        end)
    else
        Errors.log("UI", "build", tostring(err), "UI.build", "report")
        Errors.missingData("control_center", "Control Center UI",
            "UI could not be built — headless mode (console + TRO API still fully functional)", "report")
    end
end

function UI.hide()
    UI.visible = false
    if UI.root then Core.internal(function() UI.root.Enabled = false end) end
end

function UI.show()
    UI.visible = true
    if UI.root then
        Core.internal(function()
            UI.root.Enabled = true
            if UI.refs.main then UI.refs.main.Visible = true end
            if UI.refs.restoreDock then UI.refs.restoreDock.Visible = false end
        end)
        task.delay(0.1, function()
            if UI.refs.main and UI.refs.main.Parent then
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(720, 320)
                local titleBar = UI.refs.titleBar or UI.refs.main
                local hp, hs = titleBar.AbsolutePosition, titleBar.AbsoluteSize
                local outside = hp.X + hs.X < 2 or hp.X > viewport.X - 2
                    or hp.Y + hs.Y < 2 or hp.Y > viewport.Y - 2
                if UI.refs.restoreDock then UI.refs.restoreDock.Visible = outside end
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- ---- progressive report output (never freezes the UI) ----------------------
function UI.buildReportProgressive()
    if UI._reportBuilding then return end
    if not UI.refs.reportBox then return end
    UI._reportBuilding = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local parts = {}
        local totalBytes = 0
        local steps = Report.buildSteps()
        for i, step in ipairs(steps) do
            if UI.refs.reportStatus then
                UI.refs.reportStatus.Text = "بناء " .. step.id .. " " .. step.title
                    .. " (" .. i .. "/" .. #steps .. ") …"
            end
            local ok, text = pcall(step.fn)
            if ok and type(text) == "string" then
                parts[#parts+1] = step.id .. " — " .. step.title .. "\n" .. text
                totalBytes += #text
            else
                parts[#parts+1] = step.id .. " BUILD ERROR: " .. tostring(text)
            end
            -- tail-view display (full data lives in the chunk store / file)
            local full = table.concat(parts, "\n\n")
            local display = full
            if #full > Core.CFG.REPORT_DISPLAY_CAP then
                display = "…[يُعرض آخر " .. Core.CFG.REPORT_DISPLAY_CAP
                    .. " من " .. #full .. " حرف — التقرير الكامل عبر حفظ/نسخ]…\n"
                    .. full:sub(#full - Core.CFG.REPORT_DISPLAY_CAP)
            end
            Core.internal(function()
                UI.refs.reportBox.Text = display
                UI.refs.reportBox.Size = UDim2new(1, -8, 0, math.max(20, #display * 0.055))
                if UI.refs.reportHolder then
                    UI.refs.reportHolder.CanvasSize = UDim2new(0, 0, 0, math.max(20, #display * 0.055) + 8)
                end
            end)
            task.wait()   -- yield between sections → UI never freezes
        end
        if UI.refs.reportStatus then
            UI.refs.reportStatus.Text = "تم البناء: " .. totalBytes .. " بايت في "
                .. #steps .. " أقسام (العرض قد يقتطع الذيل — احفظ/انسخ للكامل)"
        end
        UI._reportBuilding = false
    end)
end

-- ---- periodic refresh -------------------------------------------------------
function UI.refresh()
    if not UI._built or not UI.visible then return end
    Core.internal(function()
        local ps = Perf.stats()
        local ss = Scripts.stats()
        local gs = Gui.stats()
        local rs = Rec.stats()
        local es = Errors.stats()
        local fs = Funcs.stats()
        local q = Report.quality()

        if UI.refs.perfLabel then
            UI.refs.perfLabel.Text = string.format("FPS %s | ذاكرة %dKB",
                tostring(ps.fpsAvg), ps.memNow)
        end
        if UI.refs.masterBtn then
            local on = Core.CAPTURING == true
            UI.refs.masterBtn.Text = on and "⏹ إيقاف التسجيل" or "▶ تشغيل التسجيل"
            UI.refs.masterBtn.BackgroundColor3 = on and THEME.greenD or THEME.redD
            UI.refs.masterBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.24
        end
        if UI.refs.footer then
            UI.refs.footer.Text = string.format("الحالة=%s | حفظ تلقائي=%s | الجودة %d/100 | ضوضاء مفلترة %d | %s",
                Rec.tlState, tostring(Store.autosave), q.score,
                Core.Stats.noiseSkipped, Caps.EXEC)
        end

        if UI.refs.dashLines then
            UI.refs.dashLines.state.Text = "الحالة     : " .. Rec.tlState
                .. "  |  مؤشرات " .. #Rec.markers
            UI.refs.dashLines.events.Text = "الأحداث   : " .. rs.unique .. " فريدة"
                .. "  |  الخط الزمني " .. #Rec.timeline
            UI.refs.dashLines.scripts.Text = string.format("السكربتات : %d ملتقط | إعادة بناء %.1f%% | طابور P:%d R:%d F:%d X:%d",
                ss.captured, ss.reconRatio * 100, ss.queue.counts.PENDING,
                ss.queue.counts.RETRY_PENDING, ss.queue.counts.FAILED, ss.queue.counts.REJECTED)
            UI.refs.dashLines.guis.Text = string.format("الواجهات  : %d جذر | %d عنصر | %d لقطة | %d مقارنة",
                gs.captured, gs.instances, gs.snapshots, gs.diffs)
            UI.refs.dashLines.funcs.Text = string.format("الدوال    : %d (مصدر %d، محللة %d) | حواف نداء %d | اعتماديات %d",
                fs.total, fs.states.SOURCE_AVAILABLE, fs.states.BODY_ANALYZED, fs.callEdges, fs.depEdges)
            UI.refs.dashLines.remotes.Text = "الريموتات : " .. tostring(rs.buckets.INVENTORY or 0)
                .. " جرد | " .. tostring(rs.buckets.REMOTES or 0) .. " صادر | "
                .. tostring(rs.buckets.REMOTES_IN or 0) .. " وارد"
            UI.refs.dashLines.timeline.Text = "اعتراضات  : " .. tostring(rs.buckets.LOADS or 0)
                .. " مقطع loadstring (مصادر كاملة)"
            UI.refs.dashLines.errors.Text = string.format("الأخطاء   : %d (مؤثرة %d) | فئات ناقصة %d",
                es.total, es.byImpact.data, es.missingCategories)
            UI.refs.dashLines.quality.Text = "الجودة    : " .. q.score .. "/100"
                .. "  [إعادة بناء " .. q.parts.sourceRecon .. " | سلامة " .. q.parts.sectionIntegrity
                .. " | حفظ " .. q.parts.save .. "]"
            UI.refs.dashLines.save.Text = "الحفظ     : "
                .. (Store.lastSave.verified and ("OK " .. Store.lastSave.size .. "B موثق") or "غير موثق بعد")
                .. "  |  ملف " .. Core.FILE_REPORT
        end

        if UI.refs.hooksLine then
            UI.refs.hooksLine.Text = "الهوكات: " .. Hooks.stats()
                .. (Core.CAPTURING and "" or " — التسجيل متوقف حاليًا")
        end

        if UI.refs.scriptLines then
            local qs = ss.queue
            UI.refs.scriptLines[1].Text = string.format("الطابور: منتظر=%d معالجة=%d إعادة=%d مكتمل=%d فاشل=%d مرفوض=%d",
                qs.counts.PENDING, qs.counts.PROCESSING, qs.counts.RETRY_PENDING,
                qs.counts.COMPLETED, qs.counts.FAILED, qs.counts.REJECTED)
            UI.refs.scriptLines[2].Text = string.format("النصوص: %d سكربت | source=%d decompile=%d | بايت محفوظة=%d موثقة=%d",
                ss.withText, ss.withSource, ss.withDecompiled, ss.bytesStored, ss.bytesVerified)
            UI.refs.scriptLines[3].Text = string.format("البايتكود: %d تم الحصول / %d مضمّن base64 | نسبة الإعادة %.1f%%",
                ss.withBytecode, ss.bytecodeFiles, ss.reconRatio * 100)
            local from = math.max(1, #Scripts.list - 4)
            for i = 4, 8 do
                local e = Scripts.list[from + (i - 4)]
                if e then
                    UI.refs.scriptLines[i].Text = string.format("  %s %s [%s] %dB ratio=%s %s",
                        e.id, Core.preview(e.name, 44), e.class, e.textLen or 0,
                        tostring(e.recon and e.recon.ratio), e.origin)
                else
                    UI.refs.scriptLines[i].Text = ""
                end
            end
        end

        if UI.refs.guiLines then
            UI.refs.guiLines[1].Text = string.format("جذور ملتقطة: %d | عناصر: %d | جزئي: %d",
                gs.captured, gs.instances, gs.partial)
            UI.refs.guiLines[2].Text = string.format("لقطات: %d | مقارنات: %d",
                gs.snapshots, gs.diffs)
            local from = math.max(1, #Gui.list - 5)
            for i = 3, 7 do
                local e = Gui.list[from + (i - 3)]
                if e then
                    UI.refs.guiLines[i].Text = string.format("  %s %s (%d insts%s)",
                        e.id, Core.preview(e.full, 60), e.instCount, e.partial and ", PARTIAL" or "")
                else
                    UI.refs.guiLines[i].Text = ""
                end
            end
            UI.refs.guiLines[8].Text = #Gui.snaps > 0
                and ("آخر لقطة: " .. Gui.snaps[#Gui.snaps].id .. " @ " .. Gui.snaps[#Gui.snaps].wall)
                or "لا لقطات بعد — اضغط لقطة شاملة ثم أعد المحاولة للمقارنة"
        end

        if UI.refs.tlLines then
            UI.refs.tlLines[1].Text = "الحالة: " .. Rec.tlState .. " | إدخالات " .. #Rec.timeline
                .. " | مهملة " .. Core.Stats.timelineDropped
            local from = math.max(1, #Rec.timeline - 8)
            for i = 2, 10 do
                local e = Rec.timeline[from + (i - 2)]
                if e then
                    UI.refs.tlLines[i].Text = string.format("[%7.2fs] %s %s",
                        e.t, e.ty, Core.preview(e.msg, 84))
                else
                    UI.refs.tlLines[i].Text = ""
                end
            end
        end

        if UI.refs.storeLines then
