            UI.refs.storeLines[1].Text = "تلقائي  : " .. (Store.autosave and ("ON (بواسطة " .. tostring(Store.autosaveBy) .. ")") or "OFF — مغلق عند الإقلاع")
            UI.refs.storeLines[2].Text = "متسخ    : " .. tostring(Store.dirty)
                .. (Store.busy and "  |  حفظ جارٍ الآن" or "")
            UI.refs.storeLines[3].Text = "آخر حفظ : " .. (Store.lastSave.attempted
                and string.format("%s حجم=%dB موثق=%s (منذ %.0fs)",
                    Store.lastSave.ok and "OK" or "فاشل",
                    Store.lastSave.size or 0, tostring(Store.lastSave.verified),
                    os.clock() - (Store.lastSave.t > 0 and Store.lastSave.t or os.clock()))
                or "لم يحدث (اضغط احفظ الآن)")
            UI.refs.storeLines[4].Text = "موثق    : " .. Core.Stats.savesVerified .. " / " .. Core.Stats.savesAttempted .. " محاولة"
            UI.refs.storeLines[5].Text = "الملف   : " .. Core.FILE_REPORT .. "  (ملف واحد فقط)"
            UI.refs.storeLines[6].Text = "لا مجلدات — المصادر والبايتكود مضمّنة داخل نفس الملف"
            local sst = Ser.stats()
            UI.refs.storeLines[7].Text = string.format("المقاطع : %d نص / %d KB / %d مكتمل",
                sst.items, math.floor(sst.bytes / 1024), sst.completeItems)
            UI.refs.storeLines[8].Text = "اختبار FS: " .. Caps.FS_ROUNDTRIP
            if UI.refs.autoSaveBtn then
                UI.refs.autoSaveBtn.Text = Store.autosave and "تلقائي: ON" or "تلقائي: OFF"
                UI.refs.autoSaveBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Store.autosave
                    and THEME.greenD or THEME.redD
            end
        end

        if UI.refs.errLines then
            UI.refs.errLines[1].Text = string.format("أخطاء: %d (بيانات=%d تقرير=%d لا شيء=%d) مهملة=%d | فئات ناقصة: %d (عناصر %d)",
                es.total, es.byImpact.data, es.byImpact.report, es.byImpact.none, es.dropped,
                es.missingCategories, es.missingItems)
            local from = math.max(1, #Errors.list - 9)
            for i = 2, 11 do
                local e = Errors.list[from + (i - 2)]
                if e then
                    UI.refs.errLines[i].Text = string.format("%s [%s] %s: %s",
                        e.id, e.section, e.etype, Core.preview(e.msg, 76))
                else
                    UI.refs.errLines[i].Text = ""
                end
            end
            local mf = math.min(#Errors.missing, 3)
            for i = 1, mf do
                local m = Errors.missing[i]
                UI.refs.errLines[11 + i].Text = string.format("ناقص ×%d: %s (%s)",
                    m.count, Core.preview(m.what, 66), Core.preview(m.why, 40))
            end
        end

        if UI.refs.capsLines then
            local names = {}
            for k in pairs(Caps.map) do names[#names+1] = k end
            table.sort(names)
            for i = 1, #UI.refs.capsLines do
                local n = names[i]
                if n then
                    local m = Caps.map[n]
                    UI.refs.capsLines[i].Text = string.format("  %-26s %s", n, m.ok and "OK" or "مفقود")
                    UI.refs.capsLines[i].TextColor3 = m.ok
                        and Color3.fromRGB(140, 220, 165) or Color3.fromRGB(235, 150, 150)
                else
                    UI.refs.capsLines[i].Text = ""
                end
            end
        end

        -- ═══════════ ⑥ v10.1 — تحديث التبويبات الجديدة ═══════════
        if UI.refs.queueLines then
            local qs = Queue.stats()
            UI.refs.queueLines[1].Text = string.format("الحالة   : %s | فاصل %.2fs | دفعة %d | ملغى تاريخيًا %d",
                Queue.paused and "موقوف مؤقتًا" or "يعمل",
                Queue.interval, Queue.batch, Queue.cancelled)
            UI.refs.queueLines[2].Text = string.format("العناصر  : منتظر=%d معالجة=%d إعادة=%d مكتمل=%d فاشل=%d مرفوض=%d",
                qs.counts.PENDING, qs.counts.PROCESSING, qs.counts.RETRY_PENDING,
                qs.counts.COMPLETED, qs.counts.FAILED, qs.counts.REJECTED)
            UI.refs.queueLines[3].Text = string.format("السرعة   : ≈%d عنصر/ث (دفعة %d ÷ فاصل %.2fs)",
                math.floor(Queue.batch / math.max(Queue.interval, 0.001)),
                Queue.batch, Queue.interval)
            UI.refs.queueLines[4].Text = "أوامر   : أبطأ/عادي/سريع/أقصى أو −/+ دقيق — وإلغاء يسقط المنتظر فقط"
            UI.refs.queueLines[5].Text = "آخر رفض : " .. (qs.lastRejected and qs.lastRejected.reason or "—")
        end

        if UI.refs.httpLines then
            local hst = HttpSpy.stats()
            UI.refs.httpLines[1].Text = string.format("الحالة : %s | مضاد HTTPS: %s (%d) | إجمالي %d | معروض %d | مسقط %d",
                hst.enabled and "ON" or "OFF (مقفل افتراضيًا)",
                (HttpBlock and HttpBlock.enabled) and "ON" or "OFF",
                (HttpBlock and HttpBlock.blocked or 0),
                hst.count, hst.shown, hst.dropped)
            local from = math.max(1, #HttpSpy.logs - 7)
            for k = 2, 9 do
                local e = HttpSpy.logs[from + (k - 2)]
                UI.refs.httpLines[k].Text = e and ("  " .. Core.preview(e.line, 100)) or ""
            end
        end

        -- ═══════════ ⑧ v10.3 — تحديث تبويب مضاد HTTPS ═══════════
        if UI.refs.blkLines then
            local bst = HttpBlock and HttpBlock.stats()
                or { enabled = false, blocked = 0, passed = 0, allowCount = 0 }
            UI.refs.blkLines[1].Text = string.format("الحالة : %s | محجوب %d | مرّ (مسموح) %d | استثناءات %d",
                bst.enabled and "ON — كل رابط يُحجب" or "OFF (طافي افتراضيًا)",
                bst.blocked, bst.passed, bst.allowCount)
            UI.refs.blkLines[2].Text = "الحجب يرجع ردًا فاشلًا فارغًا — الكود داخل الرابط لا يُنفَّذ أبدًا"
            local lastN = (HttpBlock and HttpBlock.last) or {}
            local fromB = math.max(1, #lastN - 5)
            for k = 3, 8 do
                local b = lastN[fromB + (k - 3)]
                UI.refs.blkLines[k].Text = b and ("  " .. tostring(b.wall) .. " " .. Core.preview(b.url, 96)) or ""
            end
        end

        if UI.refs.btnLines then
            local bst = BMon.stats()
            if UI.refs.btnWinLbl then
                UI.refs.btnWinLbl.Text = string.format("النافذة: %d ث", BMon.window)
            end
            UI.refs.btnLines[1].Text = string.format("الحالة : %s | ضغطات %d | أزرار متصلة %d | روابط منسوبة %d | نافذة نشطة %s",
                bst.enabled and "ON" or "OFF",
                bst.presses, bst.attached, bst.httpLinked, bst.active and "نعم" or "لا")
            local from = math.max(1, #BMon.entries - 7)
            for k = 2, 9 do
                local e = BMon.entries[from + (k - 2)]
                if e then
                    local http = e.httpUrl and (" ⇒ " .. Core.preview(e.httpUrl, 42)) or ""
                    local dl = e.deltas and (" | " .. Core.preview(e.deltas, 32)) or ""
                    UI.refs.btnLines[k].Text = string.format("  %s %s %s%s%s",
                        e.id, e.wall, Core.preview(e.path, 52), http, dl)
                else
                    UI.refs.btnLines[k].Text = ""
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function UI.reset()
    -- keep the UI itself; only data widgets refresh on next tick
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M17 — SELF-TEST SUITE (runnable in-game from the UI or TRO.runTests())
--  Verifies: queue-full rejection without data loss, huge-text chunking,
--  decompile failure resilience, save failure keeping DIRTY, Auto Save
--  OFF by default, loadstring hook capture, FPS→Light Mode, report
--  integrity. Results land in report section S03.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Tests = {}

Tests.lastRun = nil

local function T(name, fn)
    return { name = name, fn = fn }
end

Tests.suite = {
    T("queue full → REJECTED, nothing lost", function()
        Queue.CAP.test = 3
        local accepted, rejected = 0, 0
        local refs = {}
        for i = 1, 8 do
            refs[i] = { i = i }
            local tkt, reason = Queue.add("test", refs[i], nil, "self-test")
            if tkt then
                accepted += 1
            else
                rejected += 1
                if not reason then
                    Queue.CAP.test = nil
                    Queue.purgeKind("test")
                    Queue.counts.REJECTED = math.max(0, Queue.counts.REJECTED - rejected)
                    return false, "rejection carried no reason"
                end
            end
        end
        Queue.purgeKind("test")
        Queue.counts.REJECTED = math.max(0, Queue.counts.REJECTED - rejected)
        Queue.CAP.test = nil
        if accepted ~= 3 then
            return false, "accepted=" .. accepted .. " (expected 3 under cap)"
        end
        if rejected ~= 5 then
            return false, "rejected=" .. rejected .. " (expected 5 beyond cap)"
        end
        -- THE v9 BUG FIX PROOF: a rejected item was never marked as seen, so
        -- once capacity frees up it MUST be queueable again (no silent loss)
        Queue.CAP.test = 3
        local tkt2 = Queue.add("test", refs[8], nil, "self-test-requeue")
        if tkt2 then
            Queue.removeTicket(tkt2)
        end
        Queue.CAP.test = nil
        if not tkt2 then
            return false, "previously rejected item could not be re-queued after capacity freed (v9 bug present!)"
        end
        return true, "3 accepted / 5 rejected with reasons; rejected item re-queued successfully after capacity freed"
    end),

    T("huge text → chunked, verified, complete", function()
        local parts = {}
        for i = 1, 14000 do
            parts[#parts+1] = "LINE-" .. i .. " ▚ TRO10 chunker test data padding padding padding"
        end
        local big = table.concat(parts, "\n")
        local id = "TEST.big"
        local meta = Ser.put(id, big)
        if not meta then return false, "Ser.put returned nil" end
        local back, complete, note = Ser.get(id)
        if not complete then return false, "incomplete: " .. tostring(note) end
        if #back ~= #big then return false, "length mismatch " .. #back .. " vs " .. #big end
        if Core.h32(back) ~= meta.hash then return false, "hash mismatch" end
        Ser.store[id] = nil   -- clean the test artifact
        for i, oid in ipairs(Ser.order) do
            if oid == id then table.remove(Ser.order, i); break end
        end
        return true, #big .. " bytes in " .. meta.chunkCount .. " chunks — reassembled + hash-verified identical"
    end),

    T("decompile failure → recorded, tool survives", function()
        -- a fresh empty ModuleScript is (usually) not decompilable; the point
        -- is that the failure path must be resilient and REPORTED, not thrown
        local before = #Errors.list
        local dummy = Core.internal(function() return Instance.new("ModuleScript") end)
        local ticket = Queue.add("script", dummy, nil, "self-test")
        if not ticket then return nil, "queue full — skipped" end
        Scripts.SEEN[dummy] = ticket
        local ok, err = pcall(Scripts.dumpScript, ticket)
        -- targeted cleanup (NEVER purgeKind("script") — real tickets matter!)
        Queue.removeTicket(ticket)
        Scripts.SEEN[dummy] = nil
        for i = #Scripts.list, 1, -1 do
            if Scripts.list[i].origin == "self-test" then table.remove(Scripts.list, i) end
        end
        if not ok then return false, "dumpScript threw: " .. tostring(err) end
        return true, "failure path survived (errors logged: " .. (#Errors.list - before) .. " new entries)"
    end),

    T("save failure → DIRTY kept + last good kept", function()
        if not Caps.has("writefile") then
            return nil, "no writefile in this environment — skipped"
        end
        -- ensure we have a "last good" baseline
        local sentinelOk = Store.save("test-baseline")
        if not sentinelOk then
            return nil, "baseline save failed in this environment — skipped"
        end
        local goodBefore = Store.lastGoodReport
        -- inject a failing writer
        local realWrite = Caps.writeFile
        Caps.writeFile = function() return false, "injected test failure" end
        Store.markDirty()
        local ok = Store.save("test-failure")
        Caps.writeFile = realWrite
        if ok then return false, "save unexpectedly succeeded with failing writer" end
        if Store.dirty ~= true then return false, "DIRTY was cleared on failure (v9 bug present!)" end
        if Store.lastGoodReport ~= goodBefore then return false, "last good report was lost" end
        if Store.lastSave.ok ~= false then return false, "lastSave.ok should be false" end
        -- restore with a real save
        local ok2 = Store.save("test-restore")
        if not ok2 then return false, "restore save failed (writer not restored?)" end
        if Store.dirty ~= false then return false, "DIRTY not cleared after verified save" end
        return true, "failed write kept DIRTY, preserved last good copy, verified save cleared it"
    end),

    T("Auto Save OFF at boot (locked default)", function()
        if Store.autosave ~= false then
            return false, "Store.autosave is not false at test time"
        end
        -- with autosave OFF a dirty flag must NOT trigger any save attempt
        local attempts = 0
        local realSave = Store.save
        Store.save = function(r)
            attempts += 1
            return realSave(r)
        end
        Store.markDirty()
        Store._autoTick(os.clock() + 999)
        Store.save = realSave
        if attempts ~= 0 then
            return false, "auto tick attempted a save while Auto Save is OFF"
        end
        -- enabling it (user action) must trigger saves again
        Store.setAutoSave(true, "self-test")
        attempts = 0
        Store.save = function(r)
            attempts += 1
            return realSave(r)
        end
        Store.markDirty()
        Store._lastAutoTry = 0
        Store._autoTick(os.clock() + 999)
        Store.save = realSave
        Store.setAutoSave(false, "self-test")
        if attempts < 1 then
            return false, "enabled Auto Save did not attempt a save"
        end
        return true, "OFF → zero auto attempts; user-ON → auto attempts happen; restored OFF"
    end),

    T("loadstring hook captures FULL source", function()
        if Hooks.installed.loadstring ~= true then
            return nil, "loadstring hook not installed (" .. tostring(Hooks.installed.loadstring) .. ")"
        end
        local marker = "-- TRO10-SELFTEST " .. tostring(math.random(1, 1e9))
        local src = marker .. "\nlocal x = 0\nfor i = 1, 100 do x += i end\nreturn x\n"
        local before = #Rec.buckets.LOADS
        Core.HOOK_TEST_MODE = true
        local okFn, f = pcall(loadstring, src, "TRO10-selftest")
        Core.HOOK_TEST_MODE = false
        if not okFn or type(f) ~= "function" then
            return nil, "loadstring failed in this environment — skipped"
        end
        local found = nil
        for i = #Rec.buckets.LOADS, math.max(1, before + 1), -1 do
            local e = Rec.buckets.LOADS[i]
            if e and e.srcId then
                local text = Ser.get(e.srcId)
                if text and text:find(marker, 1, true) then found = e; break end
            end
        end
        if not found then
            return false, "captured chunks did not contain the marker"
        end
        local text = Ser.get(found.srcId)
        if #text ~= #src then
            return false, "captured length " .. #text .. " ~= source " .. #src
        end
        -- clean the dynamic script entry queued by the hook
        for i = #Scripts.list, 1, -1 do
            if Scripts.list[i].origin == "loadstring"
                and Scripts.list[i].sourceId == found.srcId then
                table.remove(Scripts.list, i)
            end
        end
        Scripts.SEEN_DYN[found.srcId] = nil
        return true, "full source captured (" .. #src .. " bytes), hash-verified, length identical"
    end),

    T("FPS drop → Light Mode on/off", function()
        -- reset streaks, simulate 20 FPS twice, expect light mode
        Perf._fpsLowStreak = 0
        Perf._fpsGoodStreak = 0
        local wasLight = Perf.light
        Perf.light = false
        local now = os.clock()
        Perf._onWindow(20, now)
        Perf._onWindow(20, now)
        local onNow = Perf.light
        -- recover at 45 FPS × 5
        for _ = 1, 5 do Perf._onWindow(45, now) end
        local offNow = Perf.light
        Perf.light = wasLight
        if not onNow then return false, "Light Mode did not engage below " .. Core.CFG.LIGHT_FPS_ON .. " FPS" end
        if offNow then return false, "Light Mode did not recover above " .. Core.CFG.LIGHT_FPS_OFF .. " FPS" end
        return true, "engaged at 20 FPS after 2 windows, recovered at 45 FPS after 5 windows"
    end),

    T("report integrity (TOC + manifest + quality)", function()
        local ok1, r1 = pcall(Report.build)
        if not ok1 or type(r1) ~= "string" then return false, "first build failed: " .. tostring(r1) end
        local ok2, r2 = pcall(Report.build)
        if not ok2 or type(r2) ~= "string" then return false, "second build failed" end
        local ids = 0
        for _, sid in ipairs(Report.SECTION_IDS) do
            if r1:find(sid .. " — ", 1, true) then ids += 1 end
        end
        if ids ~= #Report.SECTION_IDS then return false, "found " .. ids .. "/" .. #Report.SECTION_IDS .. " section ids" end
        if not r1:find("فهرس المحتويات", 1, true) then return false, "TOC missing" end
        if not r1:find("MANIFEST — سلامة التقرير", 1, true) then return false, "manifest missing" end
        local q = Report.quality()
        if q.score < 0 or q.score > 100 then return false, "quality score out of range: " .. q.score end
        return true, #r1 .. " bytes, " .. ids .. "/" .. #Report.SECTION_IDS
            .. " sections, TOC + manifest present, quality " .. q.score .. "/100"
    end),

    T("timeline controls (pause/stop/marker)", function()
        local prevState = Rec.tlState
        Rec.controls.pause()
        local pausedOk = Rec.tlState == "PAUSED"
        local nBefore = #Rec.timeline
        Rec.tl("this must NOT be recorded while paused")
        local notLogged = #Rec.timeline == nBefore
        local m = Rec.controls.marker("selftest-marker")
        local markerOk = m ~= nil and #Rec.timeline > nBefore
        Rec.controls.resume()
        Rec.tl("recorded after resume")
        local resumedOk = Rec.tlState == "RUNNING" and #Rec.timeline > nBefore + 1
        if prevState == "STOPPED" then Rec.controls.stop() end
        if not (pausedOk and notLogged and markerOk and resumedOk) then
            return false, string.format("paused=%s notLogged=%s marker=%s resumed=%s",
                tostring(pausedOk), tostring(notLogged), tostring(markerOk), tostring(resumedOk))
        end
        return true, "pause blocks events, markers always pass, resume restores recording"
    end),

    T("③ capture gate: nothing records until the user starts", function()
        if Core.CAPTURING then
            if not Hooks._didInstall then
                return false, "CAPTURING=true but hooks were never installed"
            end
            return true, "capture ON via user start — hooks installed (start path verified)"
        end
        if Hooks._didInstall then
            return false, "hooks installed while CAPTURING=false — gate violated!"
        end
        return true, "CAPTURING=false and hooks not installed — nothing records until user starts"
    end),

    T("④ noise filter: executor/CoreGui skipped, game GUIs kept", function()
        -- detached classification probes (no parenting into live services)
        local cgRoot = Instance.new("Folder"); cgRoot.Name = "CoreGui"
        local exec1 = Instance.new("ScreenGui"); exec1.Name = "DeltaHub"; exec1.Parent = cgRoot
        local nat1 = Instance.new("ScreenGui"); nat1.Name = "RobloxGui"; nat1.Parent = cgRoot
        local gameRoot = Instance.new("Folder"); gameRoot.Name = "PlayerGui"
        local game1 = Instance.new("ScreenGui"); game1.Name = "ShopMenu"; game1.Parent = gameRoot
        local ok1 = Core.isNoise(exec1)
        local ok2 = Core.isNoise(nat1)
        local ok3 = not Core.isNoise(game1)
        local ok4 = true
        if Core.EXEC_NOISE_MARKED > 0 then
            local anyMarked = false
            for _ in pairs(Core.EXEC_NOISE) do anyMarked = true; break end
            ok4 = anyMarked
        end
        pcall(function() exec1:Destroy() end); pcall(function() nat1:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() game1:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() cgRoot:Destroy() end); pcall(function() gameRoot:Destroy() end)
        if not ok1 then return false, "Delta UI under CoreGui not filtered" end
        if not ok2 then return false, "native RobloxGui not filtered" end
        if not ok3 then return false, "game GUI (PlayerGui) wrongly filtered" end
        if not ok4 then return false, "executor identity marks missing though marked>0" end
        return true, "executor+native filtered / game GUI kept / identity marks active ("
            .. tostring(Core.EXEC_NOISE_MARKED) .. " marked at boot, "
            .. tostring(Core.Stats.noiseSkipped) .. " skipped during capture)"
    end),

    T("⑤ single file TRO_WOWWW.txt holds everything", function()
        if not Caps.has("writefile") or not Caps.has("readfile") then
            return nil, "no filesystem in this environment — skipped"
        end
        local ok = Store.save("self-test-singlefile")
        if not ok then return false, "Store.save failed" end
        local okR, back = Caps.readFile(Core.FILE_REPORT)
        if not okR or type(back) ~= "string" or #back < 1000 then
            return false, "cannot read back " .. Core.FILE_REPORT
                .. " (okR=" .. tostring(okR)
                .. ", type=" .. type(back)
                .. ", len=" .. tostring(type(back) == "string" and #back or tostring(back)) .. ")"
        end
        local need = {
            "S01 — معلومات عامة", "S05 — السكربتات وكودها الكامل",
            "S07 — تحليل الواجهات GUI", "S12 — جودة التقرير",
            "S13 — Http Spy ومراقبة الأزرار",
            "MANIFEST — سلامة التقرير",
        }
        for _, s in ipairs(need) do
            if not back:find(s, 1, true) then return false, "file missing: " .. s end
        end
        return true, Core.FILE_REPORT .. " verified: " .. #back
            .. " bytes — sections + manifest inside ONE file"
    end),

    T("⑥ queue control: speed clamp + pause/resume + cancel", function()
        local oldI, oldB, oldP = Queue.interval, Queue.batch, Queue.paused
        Queue.setSpeed(0.0001, 99)   -- يجب أن يُقصّ إلى الحدود
        local c1 = Queue.interval >= Core.CFG.QUEUE_MIN_INTERVAL and Queue.batch <= Core.CFG.QUEUE_MAX_BATCH
        Queue.setSpeed(99, 0)        -- يجب أن يُقصّ إلى الأقصى/الحد الأدنى للدفعة
        local c2 = Queue.interval <= Core.CFG.QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL and Queue.batch >= 1
        Queue.pause()
        local p1 = Queue.paused
        Queue.resume()
        local p2 = not Queue.paused
        -- إلغاء: نضيف تذكرتين منتظرتين ثم نسقطهما
        local n0 = Queue.counts.PENDING
        local t1 = Queue.add("script", nil, nil, "selftest")
        local t2 = Queue.add("script", nil, nil, "selftest")
        if not (t1 and t2) then
            Queue.setSpeed(oldI, oldB)
            if oldP then Queue.pause() end
            return nil, "queue add rejected (cap?) — skipped"
        end
        local dropped = Queue.cancelPending("selftest")
        local okC = dropped >= 2 and Queue.counts.PENDING <= n0
        Queue.setSpeed(oldI, oldB)
        if oldP then Queue.pause() else Queue.resume() end
        if not (c1 and c2 and p1 and p2 and okC) then
            return false, string.format("clamp=%s/%s pause=%s resume=%s cancel=%s(%d)",
                tostring(c1), tostring(c2), tostring(p1), tostring(p2), tostring(okC), dropped)
        end
        return true, string.format("clamped to [%.2fs..%.2fs] batch≤%d, pause/resume toggle, %d pending cancelled",
            Core.CFG.QUEUE_MIN_INTERVAL, Core.CFG.QUEUE_MAX_INTERVAL, Core.CFG.QUEUE_MAX_BATCH, dropped)
    end),

    T("⑥/⑧ Http Spy + Buttons + مضاد HTTPS default OFF (opt-in)", function()
        if HttpSpy.enabled or BMon.enabled or HttpBlock.enabled then
            return true, "مفعّلة بواسطة المستخدم هذه الجلسة — التفعيل الاختياري محفوظ"
        end
        if HttpSpy._installed then
            return false, "Http Spy hooks installed while disabled — gate violated!"
        end
        if Core.FEATURES.httpSpy ~= false or Core.FEATURES.buttonMonitor ~= false
            or Core.FEATURES.httpBlock ~= false then
            return false, "feature flags not OFF at boot"
        end
        if #HttpSpy.logs ~= 0 or #BMon.entries ~= 0 or HttpBlock.blocked ~= 0 then
            return false, "logs written while all features are OFF"
        end
        return true, "all OFF at boot (طافية): no hooks, no watchers, no logs — user opts in via each tab's ON/OFF button"
    end),

    T("⑧ مضاد HTTPS: قرار الحجب + الردود الوهمية + قائمة السماح", function()
        -- دوال نقية فقط — لا تفعيل حقيقي ولا هوكات، آمن تمامًا أثناء اللعب
        local w1 = HttpBlock.isWebUrl("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/a/b/main/c.lua") == true
        local w2 = HttpBlock.isWebUrl("http://pastefy.app/abc/raw") == true
        local w3 = HttpBlock.isWebUrl("wss://hub.example.com/ws") == true
        local w4 = HttpBlock.isWebUrl("rbxassetid://123456") == false
        local w5 = HttpBlock.isWebUrl("game.Workspace") == false
        -- shouldBlock مطفي → لا يحجب شيئًا أبدًا (إلا لو المستخدم فعّله الآن)
        local offOk = HttpBlock.enabled == true or HttpBlock.shouldBlock("https://github.com/x") == false
        -- قائمة السماح (رابط كامل أو بادئة)
        local savedAllow = HttpBlock._allow
        HttpBlock._allow = {}
        HttpBlock.allow("https://trusted.example.com")
        local a1 = HttpBlock._matchAllow("https://trusted.example.com/api/v1") == true
        local a2 = HttpBlock._matchAllow("https://evil.example.com/api") == false
        HttpBlock.disallow("https://trusted.example.com")
        local a3 = HttpBlock._matchAllow("https://trusted.example.com/api/v1") == false
        HttpBlock._allow = savedAllow
        -- أشكال الردود الوهمية
        local t = HttpBlock.fakeFor("reqtable")
        local tOk = type(t) == "table" and t.Success == false and t.StatusCode == 403
            and t.Body == "" and type(t.Headers) == "table"
        local s = HttpBlock.fakeFor("body")
        local sOk = s == ""
        local o = HttpBlock.fakeFor("objects")
        local oOk = type(o) == "table" and #o == 0
        local ws = HttpBlock.fakeFor("ws")
        local wsOk = type(ws) == "table" and ws.Connected == false
            and type(ws.Send) == "function"
            and type(ws.OnMessage) == "table" and type(ws.OnMessage.Connect) == "function"
        if not (w1 and w2 and w3 and w4 and w5 and offOk and a1 and a2 and a3
            and tOk and sOk and oOk and wsOk) then
            return false, string.format("web=%s/%s/%s/%s/%s off=%s allow=%s/%s/%s table=%s body=%s obj=%s ws=%s",
                tostring(w1), tostring(w2), tostring(w3), tostring(w4), tostring(w5),
                tostring(offOk), tostring(a1), tostring(a2), tostring(a3),
                tostring(tOk), tostring(sOk), tostring(oOk), tostring(wsOk))
        end
        return true, "روابط الويب فقط (http/https/ws/wss) تُحجب، rbxassetid لا، السماح بالبادئة يعمل، والردود الوهمية فاشلة فارغة (403 وBody فارغ)"
    end),
}

function Tests.run()
    local results = {}
    local passed, failed, skipped = 0, 0, 0
    for _, test in ipairs(Tests.suite) do
        local ok, pass, detail = pcall(test.fn)
        local entry
        if not ok then
            entry = { name = test.name, pass = false, detail = "ERROR: " .. tostring(pass) }
        elseif pass == true or pass == false then
            entry = { name = test.name, pass = pass, detail = tostring(detail) }
        else
            entry = { name = test.name, pass = nil, detail = tostring(detail) }  -- skipped
        end
        if entry.pass == true then passed += 1
        elseif entry.pass == false then failed += 1
        else skipped += 1 end
        results[#results+1] = entry
    end
    Tests.lastRun = {
        at = os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        summary = string.format("%d passed / %d failed / %d skipped", passed, failed, skipped),
        results = results,
    }
    Rec.tlSys("SELF-TESTS: " .. Tests.lastRun.summary)
    print("[TRO v10] self-tests: " .. Tests.lastRun.summary)
    for _, r in ipairs(results) do
        print(string.format("  [%s] %s — %s",
            r.pass == true and "PASS" or (r.pass == false and "FAIL" or "SKIP"),
            r.name, r.detail))
    end
    Store.markDirty()
    return Tests.lastRun
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  M18 — BOOTSTRAP + PUBLIC API
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Boot = {}

function Boot.rescanAll()
    Rec.tlSys("FULL RESCAN requested")
    pcall(Scan.enumerateAll)
    pcall(Scripts.discoverAll)
    pcall(Gui.captureRoots, "rescan")
    pcall(Scan.scanGuard)
    pcall(Scan.scanInput)
    pcall(Scan.scanNil)
    pcall(Funcs.scanMemory)
end

-- give FAILED scripts another chance (their seen-mark is cleared so they
-- can be re-queued; rejected items were never marked in the first place)
function Boot.requeueFailed()
    local n = 0
    for inst, tkt in pairs(Scripts.SEEN) do
        if type(tkt) == "table" and tkt.state == "FAILED" then
            Scripts.SEEN[inst] = nil
            if Scripts.queue(inst, "retry") then n += 1 end
        end
    end
    Rec.tlSys("REQUEUE FAILED: " .. n .. " scripts re-queued")
    return n
end

-- Clear = in-memory reset ONLY. Files on disk are NEVER deleted.
function Boot.clearMemory()
    Rec.tlSys("CLEAR MEMORY (files on disk are kept)")
    pcall(Rec.reset)
    pcall(Scripts.reset)
    pcall(Gui.reset)
    pcall(Snap.clearAll)
    pcall(Scan.reset)
    pcall(Funcs.reset)
    pcall(function() HttpSpy.logs = {} HttpSpy.count = 0 end)   -- ⑥ سجل Http Spy
    pcall(function() HttpBlock.blocked = 0 HttpBlock.passed = 0 HttpBlock.last = {} end) -- ⑧ عدادات مضاد HTTPS
    pcall(BMon.clear)                                            -- ⑥ سجل ضغطات الأزرار
    Ser.store = {}
    Ser.order = {}
    Ser.totalBytes = 0
    Errors.list = {}
    Errors.dropped = 0
    Errors.missing = {}
    Errors.missingByKey = {}
    Core.Stats.truncations = 0
    Core.Stats.timelineDropped = 0
    Store.markDirty()
    pcall(UI.refresh)
end

function Snap.clearAll()
    Snap.list = {}
end

-- ③ التسجيل يبدأ فقط عندما يطلبه المستخدم (زر الواجهة أو TRO.startCapture)
function Boot.enableCapture(on)
    on = on == true
    if on == Core.CAPTURING then
        Rec.tlSys(on and "التسجيل مشغل مسبقًا" or "التسجيل متوقف مسبقًا")
        return Core.CAPTURING
    end
    Core.CAPTURING = on
    if on then
        Rec.controls.start()
        pcall(Hooks.install)                 -- تُركّب مرة واحدة فقط (حارس داخلي)
        Rec.tlSys("تم تشغيل التسجيل (هوكات + فحوص نشطة)")
        pcall(Queue.start)
        if not Boot._scanned then
            Boot._scanned = true
            -- فحوص أولية متدرجة (لا كلها دفعة واحدة؛ حماية ميزانية الإطار)
            task.delay(0.3, function() pcall(Snap.take, "boot") end)
            task.delay(1, function() pcall(Scan.enumerateAll) end)
            task.delay(2, function() pcall(Scripts.discoverAll) end)
            task.delay(2.5, function() pcall(Gui.captureRoots, "boot") end)
            task.delay(4, function() pcall(Scan.scanGuard) end)
            task.delay(5, function() pcall(Scan.scanInput) end)
            task.delay(6.5, function() pcall(Scan.scanNil) end)
            task.delay(10, function() pcall(Funcs.scanMemory) end)
        end
    else
        Rec.tlSys("تم إيقاف التسجيل (الالتقاط خامل — اضغط تشغيل للاستئناف)")
    end
    pcall(UI.refresh)
    return Core.CAPTURING
end

function Boot.run()
    print("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
    print("[TRO v10] boot — session " .. Core.SESSION_ID)
    print("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")

    pcall(Caps.probe)
    local okC, missC = Caps.count()
    print("[TRO v10] executor: " .. Caps.EXEC .. " | capabilities: "
        .. okC .. " OK / " .. missC .. " missing | FS roundtrip: " .. Caps.FS_ROUNDTRIP)

    -- ④ ضوضاء المنفّذ + سجل واجهات الماب — يعملان دائمًا حتى قبل تشغيل التسجيل
    local okM, nMarked = pcall(Core.markExecutorNoise)
    if okM then
        print("[TRO v10] noise filter: " .. tostring(nMarked)
            .. " executor/native UIs marked (identity, boot-time)")
    end
    local okB, nBase = pcall(Core.takeBaselineGuis)
    if okB then
        print("[TRO v10] baseline: " .. tostring(nBase)
            .. " pre-existing game GUIs recorded (names only)")
    end

    pcall(Perf.start)
    pcall(Rec.tlSys, "SESSION START " .. Core.SESSION_ID .. " (TRO v" .. Core.VERSION .. ")")
    pcall(Rec.tlSys, "AUTO SAVE OFF (locked default) — الحفظ اليدوي فقط")
    pcall(Rec.tlSys, "التسجيل متوقف — شغّله بزر (تشغيل التسجيل) أو TRO.startCapture()")

    -- ③ لا هوكات ولا فحوص عند الإقلاع — كل شيء يبدأ عند أول تشغيل للتسجيل
    pcall(Queue.start)
    pcall(Store.startLoop)          -- loop idles while Auto Save is OFF
    pcall(UI.build)

    pcall(Scan.watchLive)           -- الاتصالات تتصل الآن لكنها خاملة حتى التشغيل
    pcall(Gui.watchLive)
    pcall(Snap.startPeriodic)

    print("[TRO v10] READY — التسجيل مغلق افتراضيًا")
    print("  ▶ التشغيل     : زر (تشغيل التسجيل) في الواجهة أو TRO.startCapture()")
    print("  الحفظ التلقائي : OFF (مقفول) — احفظ يدويًا من الواجهة أو TRO.save()")
    print("  ملف الإخراج    : " .. Core.FILE_REPORT .. " — ملف واحد فيه كل شيء مقسم (بدون مجلدات)")
    print("  الواجهة        : TRO10_CC (gethui/PlayerGui) — TRO.hide() / TRO.show()")
    print("  الجديد v10.3   : Http Spy أوسع — يلتقط github وكل ما كان يفلت: http_request")
    print("                   وhttp.get وHttpPost وWebSocket وHttpService + إصلاح وسيط self")
    print("                   مضاد HTTPS (تبويب جديد): بزر ON/OFF — عند تفعيله أي رابط يُحجب")
    print("                   فورًا (رد فاشل فارغ) فلا يُنفَّذ الكود الذي بداخله، والـ Spy")
    print("                   يظل يلتقط كل محجوب بعلامة [BLOCKED]")
    print("                   زر الالتقاط (الأزرار): بزر ON/OFF مثل Http Spy — طافي عند")
    print("                   الفتح، وعند تفعيله كل ضغطة تُلتقط بكل قوتها + أول رابط HTTP لها")
    print("  API            : TRO.startCapture() .stopCapture() .save() .clear() .copy()")
    print("                   .buildReport() .rescan() .snapshot() .marker(label)")
    print("                   .start() .pause() .resume() .stop() .runTests() .stats()")
    print("                   .setQueueSpeed(sec,batch) .queueSpeedPreset(name) .pauseQueue()")
    print("                   .resumeQueue() .cancelQueue() .httpSpy(on) .buttonMonitor(on)")
    print("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
end

-- ---- public API -------------------------------------------------------------
local TRO = {}

TRO.version = Core.VERSION
TRO.session = Core.SESSION_ID

function TRO.start()   return Rec.controls.start() end
function TRO.pause()   return Rec.controls.pause() end
function TRO.resume()  return Rec.controls.resume() end
function TRO.stop()    return Rec.controls.stop() end
function TRO.marker(label) return Rec.controls.marker(label) end

-- ③ مفتاح التسجيل الرئيسي
function TRO.startCapture() return Boot.enableCapture(true) end
function TRO.stopCapture()  return Boot.enableCapture(false) end

function TRO.save()    return Store.save("api") end
function TRO.setAutoSave(on) Store.setAutoSave(on == true, "api") end
function TRO.clear()   Boot.clearMemory() end

function TRO.buildReport()
    local ok, r = pcall(Report.build)
    if ok then return r end
    return nil, tostring(r)
end

function TRO.copy()
    local okB, report = pcall(Report.build)
    if not okB or not report then return false end
    return Caps.copy(report)
end

function TRO.copySummary()
    return Caps.copy(Report.summaryText())
end

function TRO.rescan()  Boot.rescanAll() end
function TRO.snapshot()
    Snap.take("api")
    Gui.snapshotAll()
end
function TRO.runTests() return Tests.run() end
function TRO.show()    UI.show() end
function TRO.hide()    UI.hide() end

function TRO.setFeature(name, on)
    if Core.FEATURES[name] ~= nil then
        Core.FEATURES[name] = on == true
        Rec.tlSys("FEATURE " .. name .. " = " .. tostring(on == true))
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- ⑥ v10.1 — واجهة الطابور / Http Spy / مراقبة الأزرار
function TRO.setQueueSpeed(interval, batch) return Queue.setSpeed(interval, batch) end
function TRO.queueSpeedPreset(name)         return Queue.speedPreset(name) end
function TRO.pauseQueue()                   return Queue.pause() end
function TRO.resumeQueue()                  return Queue.resume() end
function TRO.cancelQueue()                  return Queue.cancelPending("api") end
function TRO.httpSpy(on)                    return HttpSpy.setEnabled(on == true) end
-- ⑦ v10.2 — واجهة Http Spy الإضافية (مطابقة لأسماء سكربت الأصل)
function TRO.httpSpyBlock(url)              HttpSpy.blockUrl(url) end
function TRO.httpSpyWhitelist(url)          HttpSpy.whitelistUrl(url) end
function TRO.httpSpyProxy(host, repl)       HttpSpy.proxyHost(host, repl) end
function TRO.httpSpyHookResponse(url, cb)   HttpSpy.hookResponse(url, cb) end
function TRO.buttonMonitor(on)              return BMon.setEnabled(on == true) end
-- ⑧ v10.3 — واجهة مضاد HTTPS
function TRO.httpBlock(on)                  return HttpBlock.setEnabled(on == true) end
function TRO.httpBlockAllow(u)              HttpBlock.allow(u) end
function TRO.httpBlockDisallow(u)           HttpBlock.disallow(u) end

function TRO.stats()
    return {
        session = Core.SESSION_ID,
        executor = Caps.EXEC,
        perf = Perf.stats(),
        rec = Rec.stats(),
        scripts = Scripts.stats(),
        funcs = Funcs.stats(),
        gui = Gui.stats(),
        errors = Errors.stats(),
        chunks = Ser.stats(),
        store = {
            autosave = Store.autosave,
            dirty = Store.dirty,
            lastSave = Store.lastSave,
        },
        queue = {                          -- ⑥ v10.1
            interval = Queue.interval,
            batch = Queue.batch,
            paused = Queue.paused,
            cancelled = Queue.cancelled,
            counts = Queue.stats().counts,
        },
        httpSpy = HttpSpy.stats(),         -- ⑥ v10.1
        httpBlock = HttpBlock.stats(),     -- ⑧ v10.3
        buttonMonitor = BMon.stats(),      -- ⑥ v10.1
        quality = Report.quality(),
    }
end

-- expose the API: _G / getgenv (Roblox + executors) with direct-global
-- fallbacks for sandboxes where _G is frozen (CLI harness / debug)
local INTERNALS = {
    Queue = Queue, Store = Store, Rec = Rec, Hooks = Hooks,
    Scripts = Scripts, Gui = Gui, Ser = Ser, Core = Core,
    Errors = Errors, Caps = Caps, Perf = Perf, Report = Report,
    HttpSpy = HttpSpy, BMon = BMon, HttpBlock = HttpBlock,   -- ⑥ v10.1 · ⑧ v10.3
    UI = UI, Tests = Tests,           -- ⑥ v10.1 (harness/debug reachability)
}
pcall(function() _G.TRO = TRO end)
pcall(function() if getgenv then getgenv().TRO = TRO end end)
pcall(function() _G.__TRO_INTERNAL = INTERNALS end)
TROv10 = TRO              -- direct global fallback
TRO_INTERNAL = INTERNALS  -- direct global fallback (harness/debug only)

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Boot.run()
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
